Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本上，ABLE达到79%的绕过成功率，其中迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例。相比现有分析平台，ABLE多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类，并暴露了先前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效自动化生成沙箱绕过规则，解决传统手工方法难以规模化的问题，显著提升恶意软件分析能力。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [180] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz 是一个基于四原则框架（P1-P4）的自主测试桩生成系统，通过LLM驱动的生成-检查-修复循环确保测试桩正确性，在23个开源项目中发现42个漏洞（含3个CVE），并识别出586个现有测试桩中的53个违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试桩自动生成工具缺乏对正确性的系统保障，逻辑错误、API误用和生命周期违规在源码层面未被检测，LLM驱动的大规模生成使得质量问题愈发严重。

Method: 提出四原则框架（P1逻辑正确性、P2 API协议合规、P3安全边界尊重、P4入口点充分性），将其数学化规约和可实现检查嵌入自主LLM智能体，通过生成-检查-修复循环在模糊测试开始前确保测试桩满足P1-P4。

Result: 在23个开源项目（C/C++、Java、JavaScript）中提交42个漏洞报告，29个被确认/修复（含3个CVE），仅2个被拒绝（4.8%误报率）。P1/P2检查自动拦截58个测试桩导致的崩溃。在70个项目的586个现有测试桩中识别53个违规（45个确认，35个已修复）。

Conclusion: 四原则框架为测试桩正确性提供了首个源码级形式化定义，结合LLM的自主生成-验证方法能有效提升测试桩质量，减少模糊测试中的误报，并可作为现有测试桩的质量审计工具。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [181] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了baseSPIDER和SPIDER两种隐私信息检索（PIR）方案。baseSPIDER采用单服务器、有状态客户端模式，在匹配最优渐进通信复杂度的同时改善了常数因子，特别适用于大条目数据库。SPIDER在默认数据库接口上运行，无需服务器特殊配合，是首个无需专用API、服务器辅助状态或协议特定交互的单服务器PIR方案。SPIDER通过对baseSPIDER的简单转换实现，该转换方法还可应用于其他三种近期PIR方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器PIR方案在通信复杂度上虽有最优渐进结果，但常数因子较大，影响实际部署效率；同时，现有方案通常需要服务器提供专用API或协议特定配合，增加了部署障碍。本文旨在设计一种通信效率更高、部署更简便的PIR方案。

Method: 提出baseSPIDER，采用单服务器+有状态客户端（预计算并存储提示）架构，通过新方法在保持渐进最优通信复杂度的同时改善常数因子。然后将baseSPIDER通过简单转换变为SPIDER，使其能在默认服务器接口上运行，无需服务器任何配合。该转换方法可泛化应用于其他三种PIR方案。

Result: baseSPIDER达到了与现有最优方案相同的渐进通信复杂度但常数因子更优，特别适合大条目数据库；SPIDER是首个在默认服务器接口上实现的单服务器PIR方案，消除了专用API、服务器辅助状态和协议特定交互的需求。

Conclusion: SPIDER通过简单转换实现了PIR在默认服务器上的可用性，显著降低了部署门槛。虽然SPIDER相较于转换后的其他方案设计更简单但客户端计算开销更高，但这种权衡在实际场景中可接受。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [182] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve是一个利用大语言模型从自然语言安全问题生成可执行查询代码的系统，通过轻量级自动提取的日志格式上下文进行引导，在133个跨5种日志类型的查询评估中，错误率比手动分析师脚本降低3倍以上，尤其在复杂时序和跨事件查询中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师需要查询系统日志来检测威胁和调查事件，但每种日志源使用自己的半结构化格式。构建按源解析器标准化日志的方法虽强大但需要持续工程投入，而直接使用grep等工具查询原始日志则要求分析师了解每种日志源的消息变体，且无法表达安全调查所需的多行时序查询。

Method: Sieve系统通过轻量级自动提取的日志格式上下文来引导大语言模型，每次查询仅需一次LLM调用，随后进行确定性执行，从而从自然语言安全问题生成可执行查询代码。

Result: 在133个安全查询（跨5种日志类型）的评估中，Sieve在复杂时序和跨事件查询上相比手动分析师脚本实现了超过3倍的错误率降低，在主动调查最关键的跨行关联任务上增益最大。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码能够弥合结构化日志查询的表达能力与直接处理原始文件的即时性之间的差距。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [183] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一种基于生成器的单次通过保护框架，通过对合成图像注入频域感知的不可察觉扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时降低被窃取用于训练替代模型的价值，实现了防护效果、图像保真度和计算效率之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 封闭权重的生成式服务通过查询API部署时面临模型窃取威胁：攻击者可通过反复查询收集大量合成图像，并将其作为训练数据窃取模型能力。现有防御需要在不影响合法用户使用的前提下阻止这种未经授权的知识蒸馏。

Method: 提出WaveGuard，一种基于生成器的单次通过保护框架。核心是频域感知的扰动生成器，在用户指定的扰动预算下注入结构化且不可察觉的扰动，使受保护图像对良性用户保持感知质量，同时降低其作为训练数据对未授权学生模型的有用性。

Result: 在WikiArt相关合成输出蒸馏场景下的实验表明，WaveGuard在防护效果、图像保真度和计算效率之间取得了有利的平衡，具有明确的不可察觉性控制，并在防护效率上有显著提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard通过频域感知的扰动注入策略，有效防御了基于查询输出的模型窃取攻击，同时保证了图像视觉质量和计算效率的可控性。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights. To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [184] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于量子隧穿栅极泄漏电流的物理不可克隆函数，用于IoMT设备认证。该PUF利用CMOS工艺波动导致的皮安级栅极泄漏变化，通过伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路生成数字响应，无需外部激励或稳定电路。65nm CMOS仿真显示熵接近1、FHD约0.5、平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），在植入式设备典型工作条件下BER低于0.000163。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT设备（可穿戴/植入式）面临物理篡改和仿冒威胁，现有PUF（基于存储器、环形振荡器、仲裁器等）因额外电路开销、功耗高或稳定性差，不适用于超低功耗IoMT场景，亟需一种极低功耗、高稳定性且无需外部激励的PUF方案。

Method: QT-PUF利用标准CMOS工艺中栅极隧穿泄漏电流（由于工艺波动导致）作为熵源；设计伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路，将皮安级泄漏电流差异转换为数字响应；无需外部激励或稳定电路，静态偏置即可工作。

Result: 65nm CMOS仿真：熵0.9999998，FHD（片间汉明距离）0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（能量19.21 fJ/bit，1.2V/35°C）；在0.9-1.3V/0-100°C范围可靠工作，植入式设备典型条件（1.0-1.3V/10-70°C）下平均BER<0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF凭借极低功耗（nW级）、高稳定性（无需稳定电路）和优异安全指标，适用于资源受限的IoMT设备认证与防伪，为超低功耗硬件安全提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [185] [Adversarial Trust Poisoning in Vehicular Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22122)
*Yutong Liu,Chenyi Wang,Ming F. Li,Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示合作感知系统中基于一致性的防御机制本身存在安全漏洞，提出TrustFlip攻击——通过部署物理对抗物使良性车辆间产生不一致观测，进而误导防御机制惩罚被针对的良性车辆；并提出TrustReflect轻量级缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作感知防御系统通过跨车辆不一致性检测和信任评估来抵御数据篡改攻击，但这些防御机制本身引入了新的攻击面，攻击者可利用一致性检测机制反向操纵信任分数，使系统将良性车辆误判为恶意并被排除协作。

Method: 提出TrustFlip攻击方法：在物理世界部署真实的对抗性物体，使得多个良性车辆对该物体的观测产生不一致，防御系统将这种不一致误归因于被针对的良性车辆，导致其信任分数下降甚至被排除协作。提出TrustReflect缓解方法：引入轻量级自反射机制，将有争议区域标记为不确定并从信任评估中排除。

Result: 在多种合作感知架构和防御机制上，TrustFlip攻击在87.7%的场景下成功将目标良性车辆移除协作，平均精度（AP）下降最高达13%。TrustReflect缓解方案将攻击成功率降低35-100%。

Conclusion: 防御机制本身可能引入新漏洞，合作感知系统的安全性需要更全面的设计考量。

Abstract: Collaborative perception (CP) enables connected and autonomous vehicles to share sensor data and jointly reason about their environment. To defend against adversaries that fabricate or manipulate shared data, existing systems employ cross-vehicle inconsistency detection and trust estimation, penalizing vehicles whose observations conflict with the majority. In this work, we show that these defenses themselves introduce a new attack surface. We present TrustFlip, a novel attack that weaponizes consistency-based defenses to poison the trust assigned to benign vehicles. Instead of injecting false data into the collaboration pipeline, it deploys physical adversarial objects that are genuine but induce inconsistent observations among benign vehicles. The resulting inconsistencies are misattributed by the defense to the targeted vehicle, causing its trust score to degrade and eventually leading to its downweighting or exclusion from collaboration. Consequently, the system loses reliable sensing contributors, degrading perception capability and potentially inducing safety-critical failures. We evaluate TrustFlip across multiple collaborative perception architectures and defense mechanisms. Our results show that state-of-the-art defenses can be significantly affected: the attack removes the targeted benign vehicle from collaboration in up to 87.7% of scenarios and drops Average Precision (AP) by up to 13%. As an initial mitigation, we introduce TrustReflect, a lightweight self-reflection mechanism that marks disputed regions as uncertain and excludes them from trust evaluation, reducing the attack success rate by 35-100%.

</details>


### [186] [Benchmarking Autonomous Agents against Temporal, Spatial, and Semantic Evasions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22321)
*Jianan Ma,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yaoxiang Bian,Jialuo Chen,Jingyi Wang,Xiaofang Yang,Shiwen Cui,Changhua Meng,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出针对LLM智能体系统的多维规避框架（时间、空间、语义三种攻击向量），构建了含2,254条轨迹的A3S-Bench基准，实验表明该框架将平均风险触发率从28.3%提升至52.6%，揭示了当前自主智能体系统存在架构级安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体（如OpenClaw）拥有深度系统级权限以执行复杂任务，但现有漏洞分析仅关注单轮、无状态行为，忽视了多轮状态交互和动态工具调用带来的扩展攻击面，缺乏对其安全风险的系统性评估。

Method: 提出三维规避框架：(1) 时间规避——跨交互轮次分片恶意载荷；(2) 空间规避——将载荷隐藏于复杂外部工件中规避标准LLM解析；(3) 语义规避——在良性上下文噪声中隐藏恶意意图。构建A3S-Bench基准（2,254条真实执行轨迹），对10种主流LLM后端集成标准智能体框架在20种威胁场景下进行评估。

Result: 该规避框架将平均风险触发率从28.3%基线提升至52.6%，且在10种LLM后端和20种威胁场景下均表现出系统性漏洞。

Conclusion: 当前自主智能体系统存在现有防御无法解决的架构级安全漏洞，亟需针对其独特威胁设计专门的防御机制。

Abstract: As autonomous agents (e.g., OpenClaw) increasingly operate with deep system-level privileges to execute complex tasks, they introduce severe, unmitigated security risks. Current vulnerability analyses overwhelmingly focus on single-turn, stateless behaviors, overlooking the expanded attack surface inherent in stateful, multi-turn interactions and dynamic tool invocations. In this paper, we propose a novel, multi-dimensional evasion framework targeting LLM-based agent systems. We introduce three stealthy attack vectors: (1) Temporal evasion, which fragments malicious payloads across sequential interaction turns; (2) Spatial evasion, which conceals payloads within complex external artifacts that evade standard LLM parsing mechanisms; and (3) Semantic evasion, which obscures malicious intents beneath benign contextual noise. To systematically quantify these threats, we construct A3S-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,254 real-world agent execution trajectories. Evaluating a standard agent framework separately integrated with 10 mainstream LLM backbones against 20 practical threat scenarios, we demonstrate that our evasion framework elevates the average risk trigger rate from a 28.3\% baseline to 52.6\%. These findings reveal systemic, architecture-level vulnerabilities in current autonomous agent systems that existing defenses fail to address, highlighting an urgent need for defense mechanisms tailored to the unique threats.

</details>


### [187] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACT是一种帕累托感知的触发式主动学习控制器，通过自适应窗口分数漂移触发器和混合采集规则，在低流行率安全运营场景中降低误报负担，相比冻结基线和周期性更新方法减少43%和21%的误报负担，同时使用更少的分析查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心面临持续的告警疲劳问题：在低流行率流中，即使较低的假阳性率也会产生大量调查负担，而聚合F1分数掩盖了分析人员的实际工作负担。

Method: PACT方法包装已部署的冻结XGBoost-Focal筛选器，采用自适应窗口分数漂移触发器，并结合阈值相对不确定性与高分采样的混合采集规则。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1两个基准上，PACT相比冻结基线分别降低43%和21%的假阳性负担，相比周期性均匀随机更新使用3.8倍和5.2倍更少的分析查询。消融实验表明采集机制贡献超越单纯触发时机，但自由运行触发器下代价是正窗口召回率下降约10个百分点。

Conclusion: 纯阈值基线虽进一步降低FP但导致BOTSv1召回率下降55个百分点，说明纯FP最小化以不可接受的召回率为代价换取更低的负担。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>


### [188] [Building Europe's Quantum Shield: The Strategic view for a Continent-Wide Quantum Key Ditribution (QKD) Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22332)
*Leandros Maglaras,Ilias Papastamatiou,Alexios Aivaliotis,Evangelos Markatos,Konstantinos Karantzalos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SEEWQCI项目，该项目通过结合光纤电缆、卫星通信和增强安全规则构建欧洲通信基础设施（EuroQCI）的数字防护盾，以应对量子计算对现有安全系统的威胁，保护电网、医院等关键服务及欧洲公民数据安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的快速发展在带来科学突破的同时，也威胁到现有安全系统，可能破解当前的加密体系。为应对这一挑战，保护欧洲数字基础设施安全，欧盟部署EuroQCI，SEEWQCI项目应运而生。

Method: 该项目采用混合架构，结合光纤电缆（地面通信）、卫星通信（广域覆盖）和增强安全规则，构建多层次、多维度的数字防护体系。

Result: 形成一套融合地面与空间通信的量子安全基础设施，能够抵御量子计算带来的安全威胁，为关键基础设施提供防护。

Conclusion: SEEWQCI项目通过构建EuroQCI数字防护盾，有效应对量子计算对现有安全系统的挑战，是保障欧洲关键服务和公民数据安全的重要举措。

Abstract: The fast growth of quantum computing can lead to amazing scientific breakthroughs while on the same time can be used to break today's security systems, raising new risks for existing digital systems. Facing this challenge, the European's Union's deployement of the European Communication Infrastructure (EuroQCI) is crucial. The SEEWQCI project combines fiber cables, satellite communications and enhanced security rules to build a strong digital shield. Its focus is to protect vital services like power grids and hospitals keeping Europeans' data safe.

</details>


### [189] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对MCP（模型上下文协议）远程服务器的认证安全进行大规模测量研究，发现40.55%的服务器无认证暴露工具，且在所有119个可测试的OAuth服务器中均发现安全缺陷（共325个），其中动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%的服务器，最终获得9个CVE ID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP作为连接LLM与外部服务的通用接口正在兴起，远程部署中客户端与服务器之间的认证边界成为安全关键但尚未被充分探索。

Method: 通过在野测量识别7,973个活跃远程MCP服务器，提出包含4大类9种具体缺陷类型的认证缺陷分类法，并实现结合被动流量检测与主动动态探测的半自动化检测框架。

Result: 发现40.55%的服务器无认证；119个OAuth服务器均存在至少一个缺陷（共325个），动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%；通过负责任的披露获得9个CVE ID。

Conclusion: MCP生态系统存在普遍的认证弱点，亟需强化基于OAuth的远程部署安全防护。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [190] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了13种后门防御方法在时间序列预测(TSF)中的失效原因，发现数据纠缠导致通道级信号稀释、任务形式转变导致训练损失退化两大根本问题，并据此提出TimeGuard防御方法，采用通道级池训练、时间感知初始化、距离正则化损失选择等策略，在多个数据集和攻击下大幅提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测(TSF)在众多领域至关重要，但易受后门攻击；然而针对TSF的后门防御方法研究不足，主要面临数据纠缠(data entanglement)和任务形式转变(task-formulation shift)两大挑战。

Method: (1) 系统评估13种代表性后门防御方法在TSF生命周期中的表现并分析失效模式；(2) 提出TimeGuard防御方法：采用通道级池训练(channel-wise pool training)作为核心范式，使用时间感知准则初始化高置信度池以缓解信号稀释，引入距离正则化损失选择(distance-regularized loss selection)逐步扩展可靠池并缓解损失退化。

Result: 在多个数据集、预测架构和TSF后门攻击下，TimeGuard显著提升鲁棒性，MAE_P（中毒性能指标）较领先基线提升1.96倍，同时干净数据性能(MAE_C)损失控制在5%以内。

Conclusion: 揭示了TSF后门防御中数据纠缠和任务形式转变两个根本性失效原因，提出的TimeGuard有效解决了这些问题，为TSF鲁棒性提供了新的防御范式。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [191] [EnCAgg: Enhanced Clustering Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning against Dynamic Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22506)
*Tianyun Zhang,Zhen Yang,Haozhao Wang,Ru Zhang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于少量已知良性客户端作为参考的鲁棒聚合方法，通过密度-based低维梯度聚类、增强聚类低维梯度生成器和低维梯度重聚类，在动态投毒场景下有效识别恶意梯度并保留尽可能多的良性梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临模型投毒攻击的威胁，现有防御方法依赖固定阈值或固定聚类数，难以适应恶意客户端动态变化的投毒策略，且常因客户端数据异构性导致良性梯度丢失。

Method: 1) 基于密度的低维梯度聚类：将梯度投影到两个最大发散维度，用密度聚类识别恶意梯度并保留聚类良性梯度和潜在良性异常点；2) 增强聚类低维梯度生成器：学习生成与良性聚类边界对齐的伪梯度，连接稀疏良性梯度异常点；3) 低维梯度重聚类：将生成的伪梯度与真实梯度一起聚类，恢复被误分类为噪声点的良性梯度。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上的实验表明，该方法在动态投毒场景下表现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过少量已知良性客户端作为参考，结合密度聚类、伪梯度生成和重聚类机制，能够有效应对未知且可变数量的恶意客户端攻击，同时保留更多良性梯度参与聚合，提升了联邦学习在动态投毒场景下的安全性。

Abstract: Federated learning faces increasing threats from model poisoning attacks, which harms its application to improve privacy. Existing defense methods typically rely on fixed thresholds or perform clustering with a fixed number of clusters to distinguish malicious gradients from benign ones. However, these methods are difficult to adapt to dynamic poisoning strategies of malicious clients, and often result in the loss of benign gradients due to the heterogeneity of clients' local datasets. To address these problems, we propose a novel robust aggregation method that leverages a small number of known benign clients as references, enabling accurate identification and filtering of malicious gradients while retaining as many benign gradients as possible, even when the number of malicious clients is unknown and variable. First, we introduce a density-based low-dimensional gradient clustering method, which projects gradients onto the two most divergent dimensions and applies density-based clustering to identify malicious gradients while retaining clustered benign gradients and potentially benign outliers. Second, we design an enhancing clustering low-dimensional gradient generator model, which learns to generate pseudo-gradients aligned with the boundary of the benign cluster. These pseudo-gradients act as bridges to connect sparse benign gradient outliers. Third, we introduce low-dimensional gradient re-clustering that clusters the generated pseudo-gradients together with real gradients to recover benign gradients misclassified as noise points, enabling more benign gradients to participate in aggregation. Extensive experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MIND datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits superior fidelity and robustness under dynamic poisoning scenarios.

</details>


### [192] [A Formal Basis for Quantum Cryptographic Exposure Measurement under HNDL Threat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22569)
*Matheus Rufino,Rafael Duarte Marcelino,Julio Smanioto Garcia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HNDL暴露概率的结构化分解框架，证明其由时间风险、密码漏洞×运营暴露项以及防御-攻击强度比饱和项构成，并指出加性评分框架因缺少交互项而无法复现该结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的加密流量面临"先复制后解密"的量子计算威胁，现有加性评分框架（如简单加权打分）无法准确刻画密码漏洞与运营暴露之间的交互效应，需要一种结构上合理的概率分解方法。

Method: 基于三个核心假设（对抗生产动力学、价值衰减动力学），推导出HNDL妥协概率因子分解形式：时间风险 × (密码漏洞 × 运营暴露) / (1 + 防御-攻击强度比)，并证明各维度的边际敏感度内生取决于组织在漏洞-暴露平面中的位置。

Result: HNDL概率可分解为时间风险项、密码漏洞与运营暴露的乘性项以及饱和分母项；加性评分框架因构造缺失交互项而无法复制该结构；各维度的敏感度是内生的而非固定常数。

Conclusion: 该框架为部分可观测条件下的HNDL暴露优先级排序提供了结构化的理论基础，优于传统加性评分方法。

Abstract: An adversary copies your encrypted traffic today and waits for a quantum computer to decrypt it later. How exposed are you?
  We show that the functional form of the answer is not merely a calibration choice -- it is structurally justified by three assumptions about adversarial production and value-decay dynamics. Under those assumptions, the HNDL compromise probability factorises into a temporal hazard, a multiplicative cryptographic-vulnerability and operational-exposure term, and a saturation denominator governed by the defense-attack intensity ratio; the marginal sensitivity to each dimension is endogenous to the organisation's position in the vulnerability-exposure plane, not a fixed global constant. Additive scoring frameworks cannot reproduce this structure because the interaction between cryptographic vulnerability and operational exposure is absent by construction, regardless of calibration. The resulting framework provides a structurally grounded basis for operational HNDL exposure prioritisation under partial observability.

</details>


### [193] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS，一个专为运营技术（OT）安全测试和分析设计的开源平台，填补了OT领域缺乏类似Kali Linux等开源安全平台的空白，并分享了其架构设计、构建和发布的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息技术（IT）安全专业人员可以方便地使用Kali Linux等开源平台进行安全测试，但支撑工业控制系统的运营技术（OT）领域缺乏类似的开源平台，因此需要构建一个专门用于OT渗透测试和安全分析的开源平台。

Method: 基于架构设计、构建和发布LINICS的实践经验，开发了一个针对OT环境的开源渗透测试和安全分析平台。

Result: 成功构建并发布了LINICS平台，为OT安全专业人员提供了一个类似Kali Linux但面向OT环境的开源工具平台。

Conclusion: LINICS填补了OT安全领域缺乏开源综合平台的空白，为工业控制系统安全测试和分析提供了实用的工具支持。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>


### [194] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep是一个四无人机编队仿真框架，用于对嵌入式微控制器进行远距离（0.25-1.5 m）电磁侧信道分析。通过三架专用采集无人机（Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码加载泄漏、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）和一架汇聚无人机实现相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）与二阶掩码消除。在ANSSI ASCAD数据集上，主掩码数据集在0.25 m处达到18±1.7的密钥排名，交叉相关性对齐可将单无人机排名从89降至21。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设探头静止且近距离，低估了空中 adversaries 的威胁。作者旨在证明无人机编队可在远距离（0.25-1.5 m）实现有效的电磁侧信道分析，突破传统威胁模型的局限。

Method: 提出四无人机编队架构：三架空间专用采集无人机（Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码寄存器加载泄漏、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）和一架静止汇聚无人机。汇聚无人机执行相干合并（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和二阶掩码消除（两路空间分离泄漏流的中心化乘积）。使用交叉相关性对齐补偿无人机悬停振动，并通过双通道CNN在汇聚无人机上进行分类。

Result: 在主掩码ASCAD数据集上，0.25 m处密钥排名为18±1.7（五种子）。针对100样本抖动变体，交叉相关性对齐将单无人机排名从89降至21。双通道CNN损失降至0.454（随机基线5.545），并在去同步数据集上提升了排名。尚未制造物理硬件。

Conclusion: TriSweep仿真框架证明了多无人机编队可在远距离（0.25-1.5 m）实现有效的电磁侧信道分析，通过空间分集和相干合并补偿远距离信号衰减与无人机振动。下一步计划是制造原型硬件。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需全局因果结构学习、无需预处理假设和因果充分性假设的局部学习方法，用于选择协变量以无偏估计总因果效应，并在理论上证明了方法的完备性和正确性，实验表明在保证估计精度的同时大幅提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协变量选择方法依赖全局因果结构学习、因果充分性假设或预处理假设，这些要求在实践中往往不现实，且全局学习在高维场景下计算成本过高。因此需要一种无需这些强假设、高效且准确的局部学习方法。

Method: 首先刻画了一个局部边界（local boundary），该边界在存在有效调整集时至少包含一个有效调整集；然后设计局部识别程序在该边界内高效搜索，避免了全局因果结构学习；方法不依赖预处理假设和因果充分性假设。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和两个真实数据集上，该方法实现了准确的因果效应估计，同时显著提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出的局部学习方法理论上是完备且正确的，能够在无需强假设的情况下高效选择协变量，实现无偏因果效应估计，适用于高维和存在未观测混杂的现实场景。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [196] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的离线决策框架，用于广告拍卖中的保留价策略选择。该框架不是输出单一的点估计胜者，而是将记录证据转化为保守决策对象，包括认证策略、统计支配替代方案以及需要进一步验证的未解决候选策略。主要理论结果给出了统一的有限目录保证，表明在同时不确定性控制和保守支持门控下，该框架能保留最佳通过门控的策略，同时仅消除具有认证遗憾的策略。实验证明该框架能将19个策略目录缩减为2个策略的验证候选名单，并在44个广告主、交易平台和地区细分中认证无伤害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记录回放和离策略评估方法只能估计或排序策略价值，但无法直接回答一个运营性问题：现有证据是否足够强以证明验证的合理性。需要一种能直接产生认证验证决策而非仅点估计排名的框架。

Method: 提出支持感知的离线决策框架，包含认证策略、统计支配替代方案和未解决候选策略三个组成部分。通过统一有限目录保证理论，结合同时不确定性控制和保守支持门控，实现最佳策略保留和遗憾策略消除。

Result: 在iPinYou实时竞价日志上，领先保留价规则在第二季实现47.66%的回放提升、40.71%的同时下界提升，第三季实现43.87%的冻结超时回放提升。框架将19个策略目录缩减为2个策略的验证候选名单，在44个细分市场中认证无伤害。

Conclusion: 离线保留价策略评估应产生认证验证决策而非仅点估计排名。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [197] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是2017-2018年撰写的一份技术报告，旨在利用在线投注策略的财富保证来推导迭代对数定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望通过在线投注策略的财富保证这一新视角，重新推导或证明迭代对数定律。

Method: 利用在线投注策略的财富保证作为核心工具，推导迭代对数定律。

Result: 成功利用在线投注策略的财富保证推导出迭代对数定律。

Conclusion: 在线投注策略的财富保证可用于推导迭代对数定律，提供了一种新的证明路径。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [198] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复第一价格拍卖中"刷单"（shilling）对学习算法的影响：刷单只改变学习者的观测反馈（输时看到的是真实出价与刷单出价的最大值），而不改变分配结果。在已知刷单分布的前提下，本文设计了结合鲁棒区间消除分支（实现动态定价率$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$）和乐观去偏分支（利用可靠后缀信息实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$率）的算法，并给出了单活跃区域情形的匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究刷单（shilling）这种只改变反馈而不改变分配的操纵行为如何影响学习算法在重复拍卖中的统计难度，揭示反馈操纵本身即可显著改变学习问题的本质特性。

Method: 提出一种结合两个分支的算法：1）鲁棒区间消除分支，忽略被刷单污染的反馈，达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$遗憾率；2）乐观去偏分支，对失败侧的观测进行去偏处理，当后缀信息可靠时利用之，达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$率。通过验证与竞赛（validation and racing）机制在不知晓正确尺度或反馈几何结构的前提下自适应选择使用乐观更新。

Result: 算法实现了在刷单干扰下自适应的遗憾上界（$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$），并在单活跃区域情形给出了匹配的对数因子下界。

Conclusion: 仅影响反馈的刷单行为足以显著改变重复竞价问题的统计困难度，且本文提出的算法能够在不预知反馈几何结构的情况下自适应地达到最优或近最优的遗憾率。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>


### [199] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于自正态化鞅构造的统计量 mHSIC 和 mdHSIC，分别用于独立性和联合独立性检验。它们无需置换校准，零分布为标准正态，计算速度比传统置换方法快25-60倍，且保持相当的统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSIC 和 dHSIC 作为退化 V-统计量，其零分布为数据依赖的加权卡方分布，必须通过置换校准来获取 p 值，导致每次检验的计算成本被置换次数（通常为百量级）放大。为了解决这一计算瓶颈，作者希望构造无需置换、零分布为已知标准正态分布的统计量。

Method: 借鉴近期双样本检验中的鞅 MMD 构造，提出两种自正态化统计量：(1) mHSIC：对两个经验中心化的 Gram 矩阵做 Hadamard 乘积后，取下三角部分的自正态化和，零分布收敛到标准正态；(2) mdHSIC：采用半样本分裂，一半估计中心化，另一半运行下三角自正态化鞅，使条件均值残差随 d 指数衰减，在固定联合变量数下渐近标准正态，单次检验成本仅随 d 线性增长。

Result: 在合成数据上（单变量维度1-500，联合变量数2-10），两种统计量的经验 I 类错误率和检验功效均匹配置换校准的基线方法，而运行速度快25-60倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法完全消除了置换步骤，通过单次正态分位数查询即可完成检验，在不牺牲统计效力的前提下大幅降低了计算成本，尤其适用于高维或多变量联合独立性检验场景。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


### [200] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于一步生成建模的保守漂移方法，用KDE梯度速度替代原始位移速度，解决了非保守性问题，并给出了连续时间有限粒子收敛界及其收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一般位移漂移场中存在的非保守性问题，该问题导致生成建模中的效率或稳定性问题。

Method: 将原始位移漂移速度替换为核密度估计（KDE）梯度速度，即核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差；同时分析了使用Laplace核的非保守漂移方法。

Result: 证明了保守方法的连续时间有限粒子收敛界，包括经验Stein漂移、KDE的平滑Fisher散度和中心速度平方的界；给出了根残差速度率N^{-1/(d+4)}和优化根率N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}；对非保守方法也得到了类似的有限粒子率但含不可避免的残差项。

Conclusion: 保守漂移方法通过梯度场设计解决了非保守性问题，理论收敛率明确，且可通过显式漂移尺寸η将残差速度界转化为一步生成保障。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Robust Statistical Estimators with Bounded Empirical Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21860)
*Valentio Iverson,Gautam Kamath,Argyris Mouzakis,Adam Smith*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“经验敏感性”的统计估计量鲁棒性度量，并针对高斯均值估计问题研究了其界限，证明了最优估计器的经验敏感性下界为Ω(η + √(ηd/n))，且该界紧至对数因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统计鲁棒性度量在刻画估计量对数据集局部修改的稳定性方面存在不足，需要一种新的度量标准来更精细地评估估计量在面对少量数据污染时的行为。

Method: 定义了经验敏感性度量；通过Efron-Stein论证技术，从均值和方差两个角度推导出下界；并利用鲁棒经验均值估计的最新结果证明上界。

Result: 证明了达到最优ℓ₂误差界O(√(d/n))的任何估计量，其经验敏感性至少为Ω(η + √(ηd/n))；该下界紧至对数因子。

Conclusion: 经验敏感性是一种有效刻画估计量鲁棒性的新度量，在高斯均值估计中得到了严格的界限，揭示了数据污染比例η和维度d对鲁棒性的基本影响。

Abstract: We introduce a new measure of robustness for statistical estimators, which we call \emph{empirical sensitivity}. An estimator $\hat θ$ has bounded empirical sensitivity if, with high probability over a dataset $X = (X_1, \dots, X_n) \sim \mathcal{D}^{\otimes n}$, for any dataset $Y$ obtained by modifying at most $ηn$ points in $X$, we have that $\hat θ(Y)$ is close to $\hat θ(X)$.
  We study bounds on this quantity for the prototypical problem of Gaussian mean estimation. We prove new lower bounds, showing that for any estimator $\hat μ$ which achieves an optimal $\ell_2$-error bound of $O\left(\sqrt{d/n}\right)$, the empirical sensitivity is at least $Ω\left(η+ \sqrt{ηd/n}\right)$. The two terms arise due to obstructions on the mean and variance (via an Efron-Stein argument) of such an estimator. We show that this bound is tight up to logarithmic factors, by employing recent results for robust empirical mean estimation.

</details>


### [202] [A Circular Chatterjee's Correlation Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22062)
*Sourav Majumdar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chatterjee秩相关的圆形版本，通过平均化响应切割来消除任意切割点选择的问题，该系数保持方向性，在独立性下为零，当圆形响应是圆形预测量的可测函数时为1，且能有效检测多重缠绕的圆形关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chatterjee's rank correlation原本针对实值数据定义，应用于圆形数据时需要人为选择切割点，不同切割点导致不同有限样本值，这破坏了圆形关系的内在结构。需要一种不依赖任意选择的圆形关联度量。

Method: 在总体层面，通过在圆形秩空间中平均化响应切割来定义系数；在有限样本层面，通过平均化样本切割间隔并简化为仅基于循环秩的简单统计量。该系数基于数据的圆形排序，保持方向性。

Result: 在非原子圆形边际分布下，独立性时系数为零，圆形响应是圆形预测量的可测函数时系数为1。证明了该系数的一致性，并推导了独立性下的无分布零行为。仿真表明该系数能有效检测标准圆形相关系数几乎无法识别的多重缠绕圆形关系（如响应绕两圈或四圈）。

Conclusion: 提出的圆形Chatterjee系数消除了任意切割点选择，保留了原始系数的核心解释和方向性，对多重缠绕圆形关系具有独特优势，为圆形数据的关联分析提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: Chatterjee's rank correlation is a directed measure of association designed to detect whether one variable can be predicted as a function of another. While the original coefficient is naturally defined for real-valued data, circular data poses additional difficulty. Applying the usual construction requires cutting each circle at an arbitrary point and treating it as a line. Different choices of cut points can lead to different finite-sample values, even though the underlying circular relationship is unchanged. This paper proposes a circular version of Chatterjee's coefficient that removes this arbitrary choice. The population construction averages over response cuts in circular rank space, and the finite-sample construction averages over sample cut gaps and reduces to a simple statistic based only on cyclic ranks. The resulting coefficient is intrinsic to the circular ordering of the data, remains directed, and retains the key interpretation of Chatterjee's original coefficient. Under non-atomic circular marginals, it is zero exactly under independence and one exactly when the circular response is a measurable function of the circular predictor. We prove consistency and derive its distribution-free null behavior under independence. Simulations show that the proposed coefficient is especially useful for detecting multi-winding circular relationships, such as cases where the response goes around the circle twice or four times as the predictor goes around once, where standard circular correlations can be nearly blind.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题，提出了**期望一致性条件**（必要充分条件），并据此设计了**期望一致性损失（ECL）**，一种无需目标域标签的无监督域适应校准方法，兼容多种校准形式，具有与ECE相同的样本复杂度，且通过理论分析和实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法假设训练与测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下失效；已有的协变量偏移校准方法依赖不稳定的重要性加权，且难以同时兼容类校准、规范校准等多种校准形式。本文旨在推导协变量偏移下置信度的校准条件并设计稳定高效的校准方法。

Method: 首先推导协变量偏移下置信度校准的**必要充分条件**，即期望一致性条件；基于该条件提出**期望一致性损失（ECL）**，一种无监督域适应损失，可兼容规范校准、类校准和顶部标签校准；理论上证明ECL的样本复杂度与期望校准误差（ECE）相同，并设计出基于小批量的可训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的协变量偏移数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性，证明了ECL在协变量偏移下能有效校准模型置信度。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致置信度失校准，期望一致性条件提供了比全局分布对齐更弱的校准条件；ECL损失无需目标域标签且不依赖重要性加权，是稳定且理论上可保证的协变量偏移校准方法。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [204] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于优化确定性等价（OCE）风险度量的风险敏感强化学习，在生成模型假设下，给出了值函数学习和策略学习的PAC可学习性完整刻画，证明了当效用函数u定义域非全体实数时问题不可PAC学习，并建立了紧的样本复杂度上下界，特别对CVaR_τ实现了τ依赖性的改进（1/τ²）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险敏感强化学习研究多集中于特定风险度量（如entropic risk、CVaR），缺乏对OCE这类统一框架下可学习性的系统性理论刻画，特别是样本复杂度的紧界尚未明确。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的模型方法，对OCE风险度量下的值函数学习和策略学习进行PAC分析；通过精确刻画效用函数u的性质与可学习性的关系，建立上界并推导匹配的下界。

Result: (1) 给出了OCE下PAC可学习的充要条件：u定义域必须为全体实数；(2) 当dom(u)≠ℝ时问题不可学习；(3) 建立了值学习和策略学习的紧样本复杂度上界；(4) 对CVaR_τ，将τ的依赖从1/τ³改进到1/τ²，但1/(1-γ)的依赖非最优。

Conclusion: 本文为OCE风险度量下的风险敏感强化学习提供了完整的PAC可学习性刻画和紧的样本复杂度边界，推进了对不同风险度量下学习难度的理论理解，并指出了在有效视界依赖上的改进空间。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [205] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个PAC-Bayesian框架，通过最大均值差异（MMD）参数化分布漂移下的泛化界限，并将MMD球解释为Walley不精确概率论中的信度集，从而实现认知不确定性量化，并推导了有限样本版本、最坏情况风险界以及测地线保持界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应（TTA）方法在分布漂移下能提升模型性能，但缺乏形式化保证来连接漂移幅度与预测可靠性，亟需一个具有理论保障的框架来量化不确定性并指导自适应决策。

Method: (i) 在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下，推导了含MMD漂移惩罚的PAC-Bayesian界；(ii) 通过MMD浓度不等式得到有限样本版本；(iii) 推导了信度集上所有分布的均匀最坏情况风险界，并进行了下-上风险分解；(iv) 建立了测地线保持界，解释核引导自适应为何能保护局部特征几何结构。

Result: 建立了漂移幅度（MMD）与泛化误差之间的形式化联系；通过信度集解释分离了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性；提供了自适应是否必要的原则性决策准则。

Conclusion: 该框架为测试时自适应提供了严格的统计学习理论基础，使得在分布漂移下既能量化预测不确定性，又能决定何时应进行自适应，从而提升模型的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>


### [206] [Aerodynamic force reconstruction using physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22111)
*Gledson Rodrigo Tondo,Igor Kavrakov,Guido Morgenthal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习方法，用于从含噪声的结构动力响应测量中重构气动载荷，无需正则化，支持异构和多保真数据，并在大带东桥案例中验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动载荷建模常依赖简化假设，导致精度受限；且在噪声或不完整数据下模型验证尤为困难，亟需一种能准确重构气动载荷的方法。

Method: 采用概率物理信息机器学习方法，结合物理约束与概率框架，从含噪声的结构动力响应测量中直接重构气动载荷，避免过拟合与正则化，支持异构和多保真数据融合训练。

Result: 在大带东桥线性非定常气动假设下的仿真验证中，预测载荷与真实载荷在均方根误差、幅值、相位角和峰值方面均高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该方法在载荷重构方面表现优异，且具有广泛的适用性，可用于模型验证、未来载荷估计及结构损伤预后等场景。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [207] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种CPU-GPU混合框架，通过批量处理分支定界节点、填充技术和轻量级自定义内核，实现大规模基数约束广义线性模型优化的一到两个数量级加速，并支持Rashomon集合收集与下游统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速的一阶方法在连续优化中成功，但未有效扩展到含离散变量、组合结构及非线性目标的优化问题（如基数约束广义线性模型的最优解验证），主要挑战包括分支定界中异构节点的顺序处理以及CPU-GPU间的频繁数据移动。

Method: 提出简单、通用、模块化的CPU-GPU框架：在GPU上批量处理多个BnB节点，围绕少量GPU高效例程构建，采用填充技术与轻量级自定义内核处理不规则的节点数据结构。

Result: 在困难实例上实现一到两个数量级的加速，零最优间隙。框架可扩展收集整个Rashomon集合，支持变量重要性分析、基于用户次级指标（如AUC）的模型选择等下游统计分析。

Conclusion: 该CPU-GPU混合框架有效解决了离散组合优化问题中GPU利用率的瓶颈，为大规模基数约束优化提供了实用且高效的解决方案，并拓展了统计分析能力。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [208] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于离散时间随机控制公式的生成建模新框架，通过线性规划对偶形式学习最优值函数，实现模拟自由的原对偶算法，生成具有直路径、快速鲁棒模拟等优势的价值驱动传输策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流、扩散或薛定谔桥的生成模型在传输路径的直线性、模拟效率和鲁棒性方面存在不足，且通常需要昂贵的模拟过程。

Method: 将测度传输问题建模为离散时间随机控制问题，转化为线性规划，利用对偶变量（最优值函数）直接编码最优控制策略，开发模拟自由的原对偶算法来近似最优值函数和相应的价值驱动传输策略。

Result: VDT策略具有直传输路径，可快速鲁棒模拟；支持条件生成、无分类器引导、未配对数据转换等扩展功能；实验展示了强性能和良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提框架在保留扩散和流模型所有优势的同时，通过直线路径和模拟自由训练克服了现有方法的局限性，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [209] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多任务算子学习（MNO架构）的近似与统计复杂度，证明共享表示不会增加整体学习成本，多任务算子学习遵循与单任务算子学习相同的标度律，并与多任务DeepONet进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多任务场景下算子集合学习的近似与统计复杂度，特别是共享表示是否会导致额外的参数复杂性负担。

Method: 对Lipschitz多算子映射类推导近最优的逼近上界和统计泛化上界；建立参数复杂性诅咒并证明对应的极小化最优下界。

Result: 共享表示不会增加多任务算子学习的整体成本，其遵循与单任务算子学习相同的标度律；MNO与多任务DeepONet在最坏情况逼近复杂度上具有相同渐近率。

Conclusion: 多任务算子学习在不增加参数复杂度的前提下实现了与单任务学习等效的标度律，且不同架构间具有渐近等价性。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [210] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对证据深度学习（EDL）中Dirichlet期望目标计算复杂的问题，提出了一种基于Dirichlet均值的插件损失近似方法，证明在温和假设下近似误差随证据量增加而衰减，并在语音命令数据集上验证了该方法在预测精度和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时实现更简单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据深度学习（EDL）提供单次前向传播的不确定性估计，但其Dirichlet期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，给分析和实现带来困难。本文旨在简化EDL目标函数，使其更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程。

Method: 将EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化问题的目标函数近似为在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失，证明在温和假设下该近似误差随证据量增加而衰减，适用于包括均方误差和交叉熵损失在内的广泛损失函数类。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上验证，所提出的简化目标在预测精度和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，且首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得了覆盖率-准确率权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化EDL目标在保持不确定性估计性能的同时，大幅降低了实现复杂度，可兼容标准深度学习训练流程；同时为softmax在不确定性估计中的使用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [211] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了均匀扩散模型（UDM）中标准桥参数化与去噪后验之间的不匹配，提出留一法后验（leave-one-out posterior）作为正确目标，并推导了去噪器、留一法后验与分数之间的精确转换。此外，引入了一种吸收态重构，将均匀扩散分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作。实验表明，留一法参数化持续提升UDM生成质量，而吸收态构造可匹配甚至超越掩码扩散，说明均匀扩散与掩码扩散的差距主要源于参数化和采样设计而非边际分布本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准UDM的桥参数化（plug-in bridge parameterization）并未优化去噪后验，而是优化了一个忽略自身噪声观测的留一法后验，这导致了插件ELBO与常规交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配，需要厘清参数化与训练目标之间的关系。

Method: 1) 理论分析证明UDM标准桥参数化实际优化的是留一法后验；2) 推导去噪器、留一法后验和分数之间的精确转换公式；3) 提出基于留一法预测器的预测-校正采样器和改进的温度采样方法；4) 提出吸收态重构将均匀扩散转化为类似掩码扩散的采样操作。

Result: 留一法参数化持续改善UDM的语言建模生成质量；吸收态构造匹配或超越了掩码扩散的性能；预测-校正采样器和改进温度采样无需额外训练即可提升推理效果。

Conclusion: 均匀扩散与掩码扩散之间的实证差距主要由参数化和采样设计驱动，而非边际分布本身的选择。代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [212] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“匹配原则”：许多看似独立的鲁棒性方法（如对抗训练、CORAL、IRM、数据增强等）本质上都在估计同一个统计量——保持标签的部署扰动的协方差，并据此正则化编码器的Jacobian矩阵。在线性高斯模型中给出了闭式最优解，并引入无标签的轨迹偏离指数（TDI）作为评估指标。13项预注册实验验证了理论预测的顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性、域自适应、光照/遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性和各向异性正则化被视为各自独立的问题，各有一套独立的方法族。本文指出这些方法共享一个底层统计问题，旨在统一这些看似无关的研究领域。

Method: 提出“匹配原则”：估计保持标签的部署扰动的协方差，然后沿其覆盖范围正则化编码器Jacobian。在线性高斯模型中推导闭式最优解（含立方根注水填充）、Jacobian惩罚的覆盖范围必要性定理、深度全局最小值处的范围二分法。引入无标签的轨迹偏离指数（TDI）。通过13项预注册实验（从经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B）验证理论预测。

Result: 13项预注册实验中12项通过（预测的匹配→各向同性→错误W排序在几何和部署漂移上的表现顺序），唯一例外（Office-31）在实验前已归因为特征间隙失效。在7B规模上，匹配风格PMH改善了选择诚实性并保持了风格TDI，而标准DPO使其退化。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是命名了部署扰动协方差这一对象、明确了正则化器必须满足的条件，并在识别该对象后提供了闭式可证伪理论，而非追求在每个榜单上的通用性。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [213] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了时序对比Transformer（TCT），一种用于金融交易序列表示学习的自监督框架。实验表明，学到的嵌入本身具有不错的预测性能（AUC 0.8644），但与领域工程特征结合后未带来显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245），说明表示与现有特征存在重叠。该方法在减少特征工程依赖方面展现了潜力，但尚未达到生产就绪水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融欺诈检测高度依赖领域专家手工特征工程，成本高且泛化性差。作者希望利用自监督对比学习自动从交易序列中提取时序行为表征，减少对人工特征的依赖。

Method: 提出TCT框架，采用自监督对比学习目标训练Transformer对金融交易序列进行编码，生成行为嵌入。下游使用梯度提升分类器对嵌入进行分类。

Result: 嵌入单独使用时AUC达到0.8644，说明模型捕捉到了有意义的时序结构。但与人工特征结合后AUC为0.9205，略低于基线0.9245，表明嵌入与已有特征存在信息重叠，未能提供增量价值。

Conclusion: TCT展示了自监督时序表示学习在金融犯罪检测中的潜力，能自动化提取与领域特征相当的表示。当前阶段虽未实现生产级提升，但为减少特征工程依赖提供了有希望的方向，需进一步探索架构、训练目标和融合策略。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [214] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于差分隐私隐私配置文件的随机平滑框架，实现对训练时（后门攻击）和推理时（逃避攻击）联合扰动的鲁棒性认证，通过DP-SGD和深度分区聚合+推理平滑的实例化验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机平滑方法分别针对训练时（投毒攻击）或推理时（逃避攻击）进行鲁棒性认证，但无法处理同时扰动训练集和测试集的后门攻击场景，因为需要将训练和推理阶段的随机机制统一到单一证书中进行分析。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私配置文件（privacy profiles）对偶视角相结合，利用隐私配置文件提供异构机制组合的数值化计算方法，从而实现紧致、模块化、端到端的复合机制认证。

Result: 在DP-SGD和深度分区聚合+推理平滑两个实例上推导出联合鲁棒性保证，并在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提供了一个通用的原则性框架，利用复合机制在更贴近真实对抗能力的复杂威胁模型下进行鲁棒性认证。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

</details>


### [215] [Optimal Guarantees for Auditing Rényi Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21938)
*Benjamin D. Kim,Lav R. Varshney,Daniel Alabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于假设检验的黑盒审计框架，使用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器直接估计Rényi散度，用于审计声称满足Rényi差分隐私（RDP）的机器学习算法。该方法给出了显式且非渐近的置信区间，并证明了匹配的极小化最优下界，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上对DP-SGD的审计结果显著优于现有黑盒方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计方法难以直接估计Rényi散度，缺乏非渐近的置信保证，且样本复杂度尚未达到信息论最优。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供理论上最优且实际有效的RDP黑盒审计框架。

Method: 基于假设检验框架，利用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器估计Rényi散度，通过类别受限的DV估计器导出显式非渐近置信区间，并推导匹配的极小化极大下界以证明样本复杂度的信息论最优性。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上对DP-SGD进行全黑盒审计，相比现有最先进的黑盒方法，在广泛隐私预算范围内获得更优的RDP下界估计，尤其在审计最具挑战性的小和中等Rényi阶数时改进显著。

Conclusion: 该框架首次实现了RDP审计的样本复杂度信息论最优保证，理论分析与经验结果均证明了其有效性，为差分隐私算法的黑盒审计提供了新标准。

Abstract: We study black-box auditing for machine learning algorithms that claim R \ 'enyi differential privacy (RDP) guarantees. We introduce an auditing framework, based on hypothesis testing, that directly estimates Rényi divergence between neighboring executions using the Donsker-Varadhan (DV) variational estimator. Our analysis yields explicit and non-asymptotic confidence intervals for RDP auditing via class-restricted DV estimators, separating statistical estimation error from algorithmic privacy leakage. We prove matching minimax lower bounds showing that, up to logarithmic factors, our sample-complexity guarantees are information-theoretically optimal, thereby establishing the first optimal guarantees for auditing RDP via DV estimators. Empirically, we instantiate our framework for auditing DP-SGD in a fully black-box setting. Across MNIST and CIFAR-10, and over a wide range of privacy regimes, our auditors produce a strong overall improvement on empirical RDP lower bounds compared to prior state-of-the-art black-box methods especially at small and moderate Rényi orders where accurate auditing is most challenging.

</details>


### [216] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型能否在实验前预测科研想法的成功性。通过构建11,488个基于PapersWithCode基准的科研想法对数据集，发现8B模型初始准确率仅30%，经SFT提升至77.1%（超GPT-5的61.1%），RLVR方法达71.35%并提供可解释推理路径。模型对表面启发式鲁棒，可跨域泛化，证明小模型可作为高效客观的科研想法验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型自动化生成科研假设，评估和筛选大量AI生成想法的瓶颈凸显。现有方法需要穷举实验验证，成本高昂。因此本文探究能否在实验前预测科研想法的实证成功，以加速自主科学发现。

Method: 1. 构建包含11,488个论文想法对的比较性实证预测数据集，以PapersWithCode基准的客观结果作为真值。2. 使用SFT对8B参数模型进行微调。3. 提出RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）框架，将评估建模为推理任务，训练模型发现潜在推理路径。

Result: SFT大幅提升准确率至77.1%，超越GPT-5的61.1%；RLVR方法达71.35%准确率，且生成可解释理由。模型对表面启发式特征鲁棒，在跨域时间分割测试集和独立构建测试集上均表现良好。
