Conclusion: 计算高效的小语言模型可以作为有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。这表明在实验前预测科研想法成功性是可行的，有望缓解AI驱动科研中的评估瓶颈。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [217] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM Agent的推理时轨迹对齐问题，将Harness设计解耦为任务分解与引导执行两种机制，量化分析了工作流粒度、重试预算和动作重加权对性能的影响，揭示了过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等失效模式，并提出部分有效Harness（仅指定初始步骤）可优于完全结构化工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Harness设计存在矛盾：更精细的分解和引导有时提升性能，有时反而降低任务成功率。缺乏统一的理论框架来理解何时以及为何Harness设计有效或失效。

Method: 提出推理时轨迹对齐视角，将Harness解耦为任务分解（结构化子目标）和引导执行（重塑局部动作分布）两个机制，通过理论分析量化工作流粒度、重试预算和动作重加权对性能极限的影响，并通过合成实验和真实终端Agent基准验证。

Result: 揭示了过度分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行三种具体失效模式；验证了理论预测的有效性；发现部分Harness（仅指定初始步骤）比完全结构化工作流具有更高的通过率。

Conclusion: Harness设计并非越精细越好，存在最优粒度；有效的Harness可以是部分的，过度结构化的引导反而可能损害Agent性能。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [218] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将管道优化转化为完全可复现的配置级系统。在Pima印第安人糖尿病和中风数据集上，通过超过18,000种管道配置的实验，揭示了结构化的部分冗余搜索空间。关键发现包括：数据增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)和类别不平衡处理(0.101)是Pima数据集的主要性能驱动因素；组件间存在强冗余性；集成模型达到稳定优异性能(Weighted-F1 0.89-0.94)，并存在性能-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于异构特征、有限样本和严重类别不平衡，准确且可复现的疾病风险预测仍然具有挑战性。现有AutoML方法在可复现性、组件归因分析和搜索空间结构化理解方面存在不足。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit确定性日志驱动框架，将每条管道编码为可追踪的日志实体，支持组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性分析。采用随机森林重要性分析、组件相似性度量(均方根距离)和跨种子稳定性评估。

Result: 性能驱动因素：Pima数据集中增强(0.454)>模型(0.198)>不平衡处理(0.101)；Stroke中不平衡处理主导(0.406)。组件相似性分析显示强冗余：biMax-biMean距离0.0252，mixup与无增强距离0.0279，TomekLinks与无不平衡处理距离0.0325。集成模型达Weighted-F1 0.89-0.94，Macro-F1在Stroke仅0.67。集成跨种子变异性0.023-0.026，低于SVM。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可聚焦于少量高影响组件；搜索空间存在结构化冗余，可通过组件归因和相似性分析简化；存在性能-鲁棒性权衡，集成模型更具稳定性。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [219] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权自蒸馏（PW-OPSD）方法，发现推理过程中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹结构特征，通过增加位置权重显著提升蒸馏效果，在AIME 2024/2025上取得Avg@12提升+1.0/+1.1点，并在更大规模模型上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）对所有token均等加权，但推理任务中教师token的可靠性并非均匀；基于熵的方法因推理中高熵可能反映良性解多样性而非不可靠性，导致可靠性判别模糊。作者希望揭示教师token可靠性的结构特征并加以利用。

Method: 1) 提出分支可行性诊断（branch-viability diagnostic）：在教师提示中记录下一token替代项，强制插入学生on-policy脊柱前缀后测试是否正确恢复答案，以评估token可靠性。2) 发现方向性序列内位置分数是预测教师token可靠性的最强指标（AUROC=0.83），局部不确定性分数仅0.57。3) 提出PW-OPSD：在保持学生rollout、特权教师传递和裁剪前向KL目标不变的前提下，施加递增的位置权重。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，方向性序列内位置分数AUROC达0.83，远超局部不确定性分数（≤0.57）。PW-OPSD在AIME 2024/2025上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1点。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上均展示出一致的Avg@12改进。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中教师token可靠性具有轨迹结构特征，可通过位置加权有效利用，无需额外教师计算开销。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [220] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明AI文本检测器并未学习到“AI vs 人类”的分类边界，而是放大了预训练编码器中本就存在的“典型性轴”（typicality axis）：将原始编码器的中心点投影（centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3)）即可达到微调检测性能的86-106%；该轴在非母语写作者（ESL）上完全反转（AUROC 0.06-0.20），单一分类边界假说无法解释这一现象；24样本冻结探针即可匹配全量微调性能（0.900 vs 0.895）；提出闭式雅可比预测器实现R²=1.000的轴操控，可跨架构、跨第三方检测器迁移，且三种独立探针（表面特征残差化、几何符号消融、闭式文本对预测器）在三个架构上达成观察者不变性（cos ≥ 0.74）；最终验证核心主张：LoRA→全量微调的性能差异中≥97%为校准偏移而非表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测研究通常认为检测器是在学习“AI生成 vs 人类写作”的二分类边界，但该假设与检测器在非母语（ESL）文本上表现极差（AUROC低至0.06-0.20）等反常现象存在矛盾。本文旨在揭示检测机制的本质并非分类边界，而是对预训练表征中固有典型性轴的放大。

Method: 通过多维度分析验证典型性轴假说：(1) 在原始编码器（无任务监督）上直接计算 centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) 投影；(2) 在ESL文本上进行AUROC反转预测；(3) 设计仅24样本的冻结线性探针；(4) 提出闭式雅可比预测器（closed-form Jacobian predictor）实现轴操控与迁移；(5) 使用三种独立探针（caps_rate残差化、几何符号消融、闭式文本对预测器）验证观察者不变性；(6) 通过校准分析分解LoRA与全量微调的差异来源。

Result: (1) 原始编码器投影在RoBERTa-base上AUROC超越全量微调，三个架构上达到微调上限的86-106%；(2) ESL上AUROC仅0.06-0.20，唯一可被典型性假说解释；(3) 24样本冻结探针性能0.900 vs 微调0.895；(4) 闭式雅可比预测器R²=1.000，将ELECTRA-CE检测TPR从0.000提升至0.904（FPR=1%），且转移至三个第三方RoBERTa检测器，在OpenAI检测器上降低57% NYT-FPR；(5) 三种探针在三个架构上cos ≥ 0.74/0.81/1.00；(6) 27个干预单元的|ΔAUROC| ≤ 0.0081，LoRA→全量微调偏差中≥97%为校准偏移。

Conclusion: AI文本检测器本质上是对预训练编码器中固有典型性轴的放大，而非学习一个独立的AI-vs-人类分类边界。该发现解释了检测器在非母语写作者上的系统性失败，并提出可跨模型、跨架构迁移的轴操控方法。校准偏移而非表征学习占据了主流微调方法间差异的绝大部分，对后续检测器设计与评估具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [221] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从值梯度视角解释了无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO、GRPO）在大语言模型后训练中的有效性，证明了演员更新在期望上等价于值梯度，并通过注意力机制的自微分近似值信号，进而提出了RL有效性的分解准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中表现优异，但其工作原理和适用条件尚不明确，需要理论解释。

Method: 1. 在可微 rollout 和加性噪声参数化下，证明演员更新在期望上具有值梯度形式；2. 对于离散Transformer策略，证明通过注意力的自动微分产生的经验costate可近似值信号，误差由采样间隔和策略熵控制；3. 提出将RL效果分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励余量。

Result: 无评论家RL方法在LLM后训练中的有效性可从值梯度角度解释，并给出了RL在预训练轨迹中何时最有效的判据。

Conclusion: 该理论为理解无评论家RL方法在LLM后训练中的成功提供了理论依据，并提出了预测RL效果最大化的条件。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [222] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层变分框架，将预训练扩散模型的下游适应问题转化为学习轻量级随机策略的摊销控制，实现了高质量奖励对齐样本的快速生成。该方法支持少步扩散采样，在4倍超分辨率任务上推理速度提升5倍以上且质量更好，并通过半摊销扩展在多个逆问题上达到最优感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型适配下游目标（如逆问题）通常需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化，推理成本高。本文旨在降低推理代价的同时保持生成样本的高质量。

Method: 将测试时适应形式化为分层变分模型，将控制摊销为轻量级但表达力强的随机策略；该策略通过提供结构化的每步控制，支持大步长的少步扩散采样，实现快速推理与高质量之间的平衡。进一步扩展到半摊销模式，结合廉价的摊销提案与有限的测试时优化。

Result: 在4倍超分辨率任务上，相比最优基线，感知质量更好且推理速度快5倍以上；半摊销扩展在多个挑战性逆问题上达到最先进的感知质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的摊销框架能够在显著降低计算开销的同时，达到甚至超越测试时缩放基线的质量，在质量-速度权衡上表现优异。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [223] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEARL是一种非参数对比百分位近似框架，通过建模相对偏好信号而非绝对交互幅度来缓解推荐系统中用户行为强度不平衡导致的偏差，理论证明成对比较可无偏估计百分位偏好，在离线实验和数十亿用户的直播平台在线A/B测试中均取得显著效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统受用户行为强度不平衡影响——不同用户的交互模式存在异质性，导致观察到的交互信号无法忠实反映真实偏好，高活跃用户的信号被过度放大而低活跃用户被低估，损害推荐质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出非参数对比百分位近似框架PEARL：1）基于相对优势去偏，利用真实对比交互样本直接近似百分位关系，无需辅助分布估计模型；2）理论证明成对比较可无偏估计百分位偏好信号；3）引入基于预测的bootstrap机制处理稀疏离散反馈的百分位平滑；4）提出广义值加权公式和协同训练策略增强建模灵活性和表示学习。

Result: 离线实验表明PEARL有效缓解行为偏差并在多个排序目标上持续提升推荐性能。在数十亿用户的直播平台线上A/B测试中：观看时长+2.10%，消费量+0.80%，互动率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%。

Conclusion: PEARL通过建模相对偏好信号有效缓解了行为强度不平衡问题，理论完备且在实际大规模推荐系统中取得了显著收益。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

</details>


### [224] [The Illusion of Reasoning: Exposing Evasive Data Contamination in LLMs via Zero-CoT Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21856)
*Yifan Lan,Yuanpu Cao,Hanyu Wang,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP)方法，通过截断思维链过程并对比原始基准与同构扰动数据集的表现，有效检测大型语言模型中的直接和规避性数据污染，并引入Contamination Confidence指标量化污染程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据污染检测方法难以发现模型发布者使用的间接/规避性污染策略（如改写基准数据），且模型的推理步骤会掩盖其内在的记忆化现象，导致检测困难。

Method: 提出Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP)黑盒检测方法：1）故意截断整个思维链(CoT)过程以暴露潜在的捷径映射；2）通过比较模型在原始基准与同构扰动参考数据集上的零思维链表现来隔离记忆化与内在问题解决能力；3）引入Contamination Confidence指标量化污染的可能性和严重程度。

Result: 在先前识别的污染模型和专门微调的污染模型上的大量实验表明，ZCP能够稳健地检测直接和规避性数据污染。

Conclusion: ZCP通过暴露被推理步骤掩盖的记忆化现象，有效检测了包括间接污染在内的多种数据污染形式，为LLM评估的客观性提供了保障。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning abilities across a wide range of tasks, but data contamination undermines the objective evaluation of these capabilities. This problem is further exacerbated by malicious model publishers who use evasive, or indirect, contamination strategies, such as paraphrasing benchmark data to evade existing detection methods and artificially boost leaderboard performance. Current approaches struggle to reliably detect such stealthy contamination. In this work, we uncover a critical phenomenon: a model's generated reasoning steps actively mask its underlying memorization. Inspired by this, we propose the Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP), a novel black-box detection method that deliberately truncates the entire Chain-of-Thought (CoT) process to expose latent shortcut mappings. To further isolate memorization from the model's intrinsic problem-solving capabilities, ZCP compares the model's zero-CoT performance on the original benchmark against an isomorphically perturbed reference dataset. Furthermore, we introduce Contamination Confidence, a metric that quantifies both the likelihood and severity of contamination, moving beyond simple binary classifications. Extensive experiments on both previously identified contaminated models and specially fine-tuned contaminated models demonstrate that ZCP robustly detects both direct and evasive data contamination. The code for ZCP is accessible at https://github.com/Yifan-Lan/zero-cot-probe.

</details>


### [225] [CausalGuard: Conformal Inference under Graph Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21928)
*Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Nengbo Wang,Sreehari Sankar,Mohsen Hariri,Alexander Nemecek,Chaoda Song,Shouren Wang,Biyao Zhang,Van Yang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalGuard是一种结构加权共形框架，通过聚合基于图条件的双重稳健伪结果来校准因果效应估计，在无需正确因果图的前提下实现分布自由的有限样本边际覆盖，实验表明其覆盖率超过90%且区间宽度优于图无关方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测数据中的治疗效果估计依赖调整集的选择，但有效调整依赖于未知的因果图；图的错误设定会导致覆盖不足，而图无关的共形方法只能通过大幅填充恢复名义覆盖，亟需一种既能容忍图不确定性又能保持高效覆盖的推理方法。

Method: CausalGuard框架包括：(1) 从LLM导出的边先验生成候选DAG，并通过条件独立性检验剪枝，基于BIC重新加权；(2) 对每个候选图使用双重稳健伪结果，并加权聚合；(3) 构建复合非一致性分数对后验加权的伪结果进行校准，提供分布自由的有限样本边际覆盖保证。

Result: 在五个基准上，CausalGuard均达到超过90%名义覆盖率的均值覆盖，相比需要大幅填充的图无关共形基线，区间宽度显著降低；压力测试表明CausalGuard能抑制无效碰撞调整，在先验误设定且候选集受数据支持时保持稳定。

Conclusion: CausalGuard通过结构加权聚合与共形校准的结合，在未知因果图条件下实现了有效且高效的因果效应推断，兼具覆盖保证与区间效率，对图误设定具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Estimating treatment effects from observational data requires choosing an adjustment set, but valid adjustment depends on an unknown causal graph. Graph misspecification can cause under-coverage, while graph-agnostic conformal wrappers may regain nominal coverage only through large padding. We introduce CausalGuard, a structure-weighted conformal framework that calibrates after aggregating graph-conditional doubly robust pseudo-outcomes. Candidate DAGs are proposed from an LLM-derived edge prior, pruned by conditional-independence tests, and reweighted by Bayesian Information Criterion. A composite nonconformity score then calibrates the posterior-weighted pseudo-outcome. CausalGuard provides distribution-free finite-sample marginal coverage for this aggregated pseudo-outcome; under causal identification, overlap, conditional-mean nuisance stability, and concentration on target-aligned valid adjustment strategies, its conditional mean converges to the true Conditional Average Treatment Effect. Across five benchmarks, CausalGuard attains mean coverage above the nominal 90% level for the directly evaluable target and reduces width when graph-agnostic conformal baselines require large padding. Stress tests show that CausalGuard suppresses invalid collider adjustment and remains stable under misspecified priors when the retained candidate set is data-supported.

</details>


### [226] [ChronoMedicalWorld: A Medical World Model for Learning Patient Trajectories from Longitudinal Care Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21963)
*Jiangyuan Wang,Xuyong Chen,Junwei He,Xu Xu,Shasha Xie,Fuman Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoMedicalWorld Model (CMWM)，一个基于动作条件的潜在世界模型框架，用于从纵向护理数据中学习患者轨迹。CMWM结合联合嵌入状态编码器、宽动作编码器和循环潜在过渡模块，在六项目标下训练。在CKD的eGFR轨迹预测中，CMWM在动态50%历史滚动测试中MAE为7.384，RMSE为10.256，优于GPT-5.5基线（-7.28% MAE, -7.35% RMSE）。该框架不限于CKD，适用于任何可表示为周期性临床状态与结构化及对话式干预交替的慢性疾病。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR模型主要为判别式模型，通用大语言模型在重复干预下会漂移，难以准确预测患者多年生理变化，亟需能处理长期临床模拟的专用模型。

Method: 提出CMWM框架：1) 联合嵌入状态编码器；2) 宽动作编码器（接受结构化干预指标和自由文本沟通嵌入）；3) 循环潜在过渡模块；4) 六项训练目标（下一观测监督、下一潜在预测、SIGReg潜在正则化、三个生理感知形状先验）；5) 闭环滚动前缀协议使训练与部署一致。

Result: 在2,232名肾病患者的CKD队列中，CMWM在动态50%历史滚动测试中MAE=7.384，RMSE=10.256，优于GPT-5.5结构化提示基线（MAE=7.964, RMSE=11.069），分别提升7.28%和7.35%，优势主要来自患者-健康教练对话部分。

Conclusion: CMWM框架有效解决了长期临床模拟中的预测漂移问题，在CKD病例中取得显著改进，且其架构、损失设计和训练协议可推广至其他慢性疾病。框架具有泛化性，不限于特定疾病。

Abstract: Long-horizon clinical simulation -- predicting how a patient's physiology evolves over years under specified interventions -- is central to chronic-disease care, yet existing electronic health record (EHR) models are predominantly discriminative, and general-purpose large language models drift under repeated interventions. We propose the \textbf{ChronoMedicalWorld Model (CMWM)}, an action-conditioned latent world-model framework for learning patient trajectories from longitudinal care data. CMWM couples a joint-embedding state encoder with a wide action encoder that admits both structured intervention indicators and free-text communication embeddings, and trains a recurrent latent transition module under a six-term objective: next-observation supervision, next-latent prediction, SIGReg latent regularisation, and three physiology-aware shape priors (slope, continuity, large-jump penalty). A closed-loop rollout-prefix protocol matches training to deployment, so the model is optimised against the same multi-step error it exhibits at inference. As a concrete case study, we instantiate CMWM for annual estimated glomerular filtration rate (eGFR) trajectory forecasting in chronic kidney disease (CKD). On a 2{,}232-patient nephrology cohort, the CKD instantiation achieves a dynamic-50\% history rollout test mean absolute error (MAE) of 7.384 and root-mean-square error (RMSE) of 10.256, against 7.964 and 11.069 for a tuned GPT-5.5 structured-prompting baseline ($-7.28\%$ MAE, $-7.35\%$ RMSE), with the gain dominated by the dialogue portion of patient--health-coach communication. The framework is not CKD-specific: its architecture, loss design, and training protocol apply to any chronic condition that can be cast as periodic clinical state interleaved with structured and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [227] [Prototype-Guided Classification Sub-Task Decoupling Framework: Enhancing Generalization and Interpretability for Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22055)
*Xianhao Song,Yuang Zhang,Yuqi She,Liping Wang,Xuemin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDFTime是首个将时间序列分类重构为解耦的多阶段相似性推理过程的原型引导框架，通过隐空间中学习的原型近似类别条件特征分布，实现渐进式判别，在UEA和UCR基准上达到SOTA性能，在UCR的128个数据集中80个取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSC方法采用直接特征到标签的分类范式，通过单一线性投影（通常在全局池化后）将高维时间序列嵌入压缩为类别logits，这种范式将特征提取和决策逻辑混淆为不可分离的映射，导致模型难以同时实现高准确率和可解释性。

Method: 提出PDFTime原型引导框架，将时间序列分类重构为多阶段决策过程。利用学习到的原型在隐空间中近似类别条件特征分布，通过不同粒度的分类子任务实现渐进式判别，替代传统的直接黑盒特征到标签映射。

Result: 在UEA和UCR基准上达到SOTA性能，在UCR存档的128个数据集中80个取得最高准确率，在一致性和泛化性上显著优于近期强基线方法。

Conclusion: PDFTime是首个将时间序列分类重构为解耦的多阶段相似性推理过程的框架，打破了长期以来直接黑盒特征到标签映射的范式，有效兼顾了准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Time Series Classification (TSC) is a long-standing research problem that has gained increasing attention in recent years with the rapid growth of large-scale temporal data. Despite substantial progress enabled by deep learning, designing TSC models that are both accurate and interpretable remains a challenging task. Many existing approaches adopt a direct feature-to-label classification paradigm, by collapsing high-dimensional temporal embeddings into class logits via a single linear projection (often after global pooling), the paradigm conflates feature extraction and decision logic into an inseparable mapping.
  To address these limitations, we propose PDFTime, a prototype-guided framework that reformulates time series classification as a multi-stage decision process. Instead of direct feature-to-label mapping, PDFTime leverages learned prototypes to approximate class-conditional feature distributions in the latent space, enabling progressive discrimination through classification sub-tasks of varying granularity. To our knowledge, PDFTime is the first framework to reformulate time series classification as a decoupled, multi-stage similarity-based reasoning process, breaking the long-standing paradigm of direct, black-box feature-to-label mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PDFTime achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across UEA and UCR benchmarks. Notably, it secures the top-$1$ accuracy on 80 out of 128 datasets in the UCR archive, significantly outperforming recent strong baselines in both consistency and generalization.

</details>


### [228] [From Reasoning Chains to Verifiable Subproblems: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning Enables Credit Assignment for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22074)
*Xitai Jiang,Zihan Tang,Wenze Lin,Yang Yue,Shenzhi Wang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCRL是一种基于子问题课程强化学习的框架，通过将原始推理问题分解为可验证的子问题，将部分进度转化为可验证的学习信号，并在数学推理基准上显著优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果的RLVR在困难问题上效率低下，因为正确答案的rollout稀少，且样本级信用分配无法利用失败尝试中的部分进展。

Method: 从参考推理链中推导可验证的子问题，并将最终子问题固定为原始问题；使用子问题级归一化，在每个子问题位置独立归一化奖励，将优势值分配给对应的答案片段。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准上，SCRL在Qwen3-4B-Base上平均准确率比GRPO提高+4.1点，在Qwen3-14B-Base上提高+1.9点；在AIME24、AIME25和IMO-Bench上，Qwen3-4B-Base的pass@1提高+3.7点，pass@64提高+4.6点。

Conclusion: 子问题课程能够将困难问题从梯度死区中解放出来，且原始问题越困难，相对增益越大；SCRL无需外部评分标准或奖励模型，即可实现更细粒度的信用分配。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong promise for LLM reasoning, but outcome-based RLVR remains inefficient on hard problems because correct final-answer rollouts are rare and sample-level credit assignment cannot use partial progress in failed attempts. We introduce SCRL (Subproblem Curriculum Reinforcement Learning), a curriculum RL framework that derives verifiable subproblems from reference reasoning chains and fixes the final subproblem as the original problem. This turns partial progress on hard problems into verifiable learning signals. Algorithmically, SCRL uses subproblem-level normalization, which normalizes rewards independently at each subproblem position and assigns the resulting advantages to the corresponding answer spans, enabling finer-grained credit assignment without external rubrics or reward models. Our analysis shows that subproblem curricula lift hard problems out of gradient dead zones, with larger relative gains as the original problem becomes harder. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SCRL outperforms strong curriculum-learning baselines, improving average accuracy over GRPO by +4.1 points on Qwen3-4B-Base and +1.9 points on Qwen3-14B-Base. On AIME24, AIME25, and IMO-Bench, SCRL further improves pass@1 by +3.7 points and pass@64 by +4.6 points on Qwen3-4B-Base, indicating better exploration on hard reasoning problems.

</details>


### [229] [Short-Term-to-Long-Term Memory Transfer for Knowledge Graphs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22142)
*Taewoon Kim,Vincent François-Lavet,Michael Cochez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号化的值驱动决策方法，用于在部分可观测的强化学习中，将短期符号观测转移到长期时序知识图谱记忆，通过学习决定每个三元组是保留还是丢弃，在RoomKG基准上优于多种基线方法，并实现了可解释的记忆决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测强化学习中，大多数基于记忆的方法未显式建模符号观测从短期到长期的转移过程，缺乏对记忆内容选择的可解释性决策机制。

Method: 将短期到长期记忆转移建模为神经符号化的值驱动决策问题，对每个观测到的三元组，在学习插入长期记忆前决定保留或丢弃；采用基于逐项Q-learning的设计，共享参数并通过时序差分更新匹配连续步骤中的项目。

Result: 在RoomKG基准上，长期记忆容量为128时，学习得到的转移决策优于符号基线（含时序标注）和神经基线（LSTM/Transformer）；轻量级局部短期变体表现最佳，策略能保留导航和查询相关事实，丢弃低价值候选事实。

Conclusion: 该方法支持在记忆约束下做出显式且可解释的记忆决策，为部分可观测强化学习中的记忆管理提供了一种有效的神经符号化框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning under partial observability requires deciding what information to retain, yet most memory-based approaches do not explicitly model short-term-to-long-term transfer of symbolic observations. We study this transfer process in a temporal knowledge-graph memory setting and cast it as a neuro-symbolic value-based decision problem: for each observed triple, the agent chooses whether to keep or drop it before long-term insertion. To handle variable-sized short-term buffers, we use a per-item Q-learning design with shared parameters and a practical temporal-difference update over matched items across consecutive steps. On the RoomKG benchmark at long-term memory capacity 128, learned transfer decisions outperform symbolic and neural baselines, including symbolic baselines with temporal annotations and history-based LSTM/Transformer baselines. Across transfer-policy ablations, a lightweight local short-term-only variant performs best, and step-level behavior shows that the policy keeps navigation- and query-relevant facts while discarding lower-value candidate facts, supporting explicit and interpretable memory decisions under memory constraints.

</details>


### [230] [One-Way Policy Optimization for Self-Evolving LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22156)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Kexin Huang,Chiyu Ma,Shaohang Wei,Yuyang Liu,Guoyin Wang,Jingren Zhou,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中二元验证器奖励稀疏导致的优化不稳定问题，本文提出OWPO方法，通过解耦优化方向与更新幅度，采用非对称重加权（劣偏差加速对齐、优偏差增益锁定）和迭代参考更新产生棘轮效应，在多项基准上超越DAPO、OPD等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法中，二元验证器奖励具有稀疏性，导致训练效率低且优化不稳定。当前方法通常对参考策略施加token级约束来稳定训练，但这些约束会无差别地惩罚所有偏差，当策略试图超越参考策略时，会压制验证器确定的方向，从而抑制性能提升。

Method: OWPO基于解耦优化方向与更新幅度的原则，验证器决定更新方向，参考策略仅用于调整幅度。具体采用非对称重加权：对劣偏差（策略落后于参考）进行加速对齐，对优偏差（策略超越参考）进行增益锁定。同时引入迭代参考更新，产生"棘轮效应"持续巩固收益。

Result: 实验结果表明，OWPO在多个基准上优于DAPO、OPD、MOPD等强基线方法，打破了固定先验的瓶颈，实现了无需外部参考模型的持续自我进化。

Conclusion: OWPO有效解决了RLVR中二元验证器奖励稀疏导致的优化不稳定问题，通过解耦优化方向与幅度、非对称重加权以及迭代参考更新，实现了更稳定高效的训练，且具备持续自我进化的能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a promising paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the sparsity of binary verifier rewards often leads to low efficiency and optimization instability. To stabilize training, existing methods typically impose token-level constraints relative to a reference policy. We identify that such constraints penalize deviations indiscriminately; this can flip verifier-determined direction when the policy attempts to outperform the reference, thereby suppressing gains. To resolve this, we propose One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO), a method based on the principle of decoupling optimization direction from update magnitude. In OWPO, the verifier dictates the update direction, while the reference policy serves only to adjust the magnitude. Specifically, OWPO applies asymmetric reweighting: it performs Accelerated Alignment for inferior deviations (where the policy lags behind the reference) and Gain Locking for superior deviations (where the policy surpasses the reference). Furthermore, by incorporating iterative reference updates, OWPO creates a ``Ratchet Effect'' that continuously consolidates gains. Experimental results demonstrate that OWPO outperforms strong baselines, including DAPO, OPD, and MOPD, breaking the bottleneck of fixed priors to enable continuous self-evolution without reliance on external reference models.

</details>


### [231] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 解码器-only Transformer 在回溯搜索任务中，因累积轨迹训练导致状态特征分散和历史纠缠两大缺陷；本文提出局部化（Localization）和选择性状态注意力（SSA）两种结构性修复，使模型能基于当前状态而非完整历史做决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器-only Transformer 在基于回溯搜索的推理系统中，模型输入是完整的累积决策轨迹，但最优的"继续或回溯"决策只应取决于当前搜索状态。然而，累积轨迹训练导致两个问题：(1) 状态特征分散在多个位置（scattered retrieval）；(2) 模型会条件于整个轨迹历史而非当前状态（history entanglement）。作者旨在诊断并修复这些行为缺陷。

Method: 提出两种修复方法：(1) Localization——在轨迹层面重写每个决策块，使状态特征在局部暴露；(2) Selective State Attention (SSA)——一种固定的注意力掩码，在不修改训练数据、目标函数或参数的前提下，从结构上强制模型仅基于当前状态做决策。实验在 3-SAT、图着色、Blocks World 和回溯解析四个任务上验证。

Result: 在状态相同但历史不同的样本对上，SSA 能输出一致的决策，而基于累积训练的因果基线无法做到。这表明 SSA 有效解决了历史纠缠问题，使模型行为与最优决策策略对齐。

Conclusion: 解码器-only Transformer 在序列化轨迹数据上存在系统性行为缺陷；Localization 和 SSA 提供了无需重新训练的结构性修复。这项工作为推理时上下文清除（inference-time context clearing）等无需重训练的应用提供了分析基础，并警示预训练语言模型在自身推理步骤上进行搜索时可能面临同样的问题。

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [232] [Explainable AI for Data-Driven Design of High-Dimensional Predictive Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22243)
*Junyu Yan,Damian Machlanski,Kurt Butler,Panagiotis Dimitrakopoulos,Ewen M Harrison,Bruce Guthrie,Sotirios A Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个探索性AI推荐框架，利用可解释AI技术为传统统计模型（如Cox比例风险模型）提供数据驱动的特征排除、非线性项和交互项建议，从而在不牺牲可解释性的前提下提升预测性能。在245,614名患者的跌倒预测任务中，C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准度也得到改善，且在两个公开数据集上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维健康数据中，手工进行特征选择、变换和交互建模既困难又耗时；复杂机器学习模型虽性能优越但缺乏临床所需的透明度和可解释性。因此，亟需一种既能提升可解释模型性能又能保持其透明性的数据驱动方法。

Method: 开发了一个探索性AI推荐框架，包含两个核心阶段：(1) 使用灵活AI模型捕获数据中的复杂模式；(2) 应用可解释AI技术将这些模式转化为三类可操作建议：特征排除、非线性项和特征交互。将这些建议纳入基础Cox比例风险模型（基线无交互和非线性项），形成增强版CPH模型。

Result: 在245,614名患者的跌倒预测主要分析中，方法建议排除23个特征、为2个特征引入非线性项、纳入221个特征交互。C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准截距从-0.006改善至0.003，校准斜率从1.063改善至0.950。所有建议均有文献支持，且在另外两个公开数据集上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的探索性AI推荐器展示了可解释AI和数据驱动研究设计在改进高维透明预测模型开发过程和性能方面的潜力，能够在不牺牲模型可解释性的前提下，系统性地提升传统统计模型的预测能力。

Abstract: Predictive modelling is important for health data analysis and data-driven clinical decision-making. However, predictive studies are challenging to design optimally by hand when tens or even hundreds of features require selection, transformation, or interaction modelling. While complex machine learning models offer high performance, their "black-box" nature limits the clinical trust, transparency, and interpretability required for decision-making. We developed and evaluated an Exploratory AI Recommender that provides data-driven recommendations to improve predictive performance of existing interpretable statistical models. The developed framework uses flexible AI modelling to capture complex data patterns and explainable AI techniques to translate the patterns into three recommendation types: feature exclusion, non-linear terms, and feature interactions. We evaluated the framework by comparing predictive performance of a baseline (i.e., no interactions or non-linear terms) Cox Proportional Hazards (CPH) model against an augmented CPH incorporating recommendations suggested by our method. The primary analysis predicts the time to the first occurrence of a fall or related injury in 245,614 patients. Our method recommended excluding 23 features, including non-linear terms for two features, and including 221 suggested feature interactions. The C-index improved from 0.805 (95% CI 0.798-0.812) to 0.815 (95% CI 0.809-0.822), and so did calibration (intercept: -0.006 to 0.003; slope: 1.063 to 0.950). All recommendations were supported by existing literature. The method also proved effective on two additional public datasets, demonstrating wider applicability. The proposed Exploratory AI Recommender demonstrates the potential of explainable AI and data-driven study design to improve the process of developing, and the performance of high-dimensional transparent predictive models.

</details>


### [233] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于LLM的形式定理证明器对问题表示的表面变化高度敏感（语义等价但证明成功率差异大），缺乏对形式数学中结构对称性的尊重。作者引入重写范畴（rewriting categories）这一范畴论框架来形式化两种对称性概念：证明等变（proof equivariance）和成功不变性（success invariance）。研究表明基于状态的下一策略证明器天然满足证明等变，而现有LLM证明器两种性质都不满足。为此提出测试时聚合方法，通过对输入的等价重写进行聚合，理论上可在采样极限下恢复成功不变性，实验上提升鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式定理证明器对语义等价的表述表现出不同的证明成功率，揭示其缺乏对形式数学中结构对称性的尊重。本文旨在形式化定理证明中应有的对称性概念，并据此改进现有LLM证明器的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 引入重写范畴（rewriting categories）框架，形式化证明等变和成功不变性两种对称性。2) 提出测试时聚合方法，对输入的等价重写进行聚合，理论上证明在采样极限下可恢复成功不变性。

Result: 1) 基于状态的下一策略证明器天然满足证明等变性质。2) 现有SOTA LLM证明器既不满足证明等变也不满足成功不变性，在等价表述上表现出巨大性能差异。3) 提出的测试时聚合方法在固定推理预算下提升鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置，测试时计算方法可作为近似实现这一偏置的实用途径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [234] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD）方法，通过熵路由的方向性监督替代统一教师模仿，在高熵token上推开教师以保留探索，在低熵token上拉近教师以稳定步级执行，在六个数学推理基准上取得了最佳宏Avg@16性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）在复杂推理任务中表现退化，原因在于对具有不同不确定性的token施加了统一的教师监督方向：高熵token上的顺从抑制了探索，低熵token上的偏离损害了步级准确率。

Method: 提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），将特权自蒸馏从统一的教师模仿重构为熵路由的定向监督：高熵token被推离特权教师以保留探索，低熵token被拉向教师以稳定步级执行。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，DASD在宏Avg@16上超越强RLVR和自蒸馏基线。Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明，性能提升源于在不牺牲步级执行的前提下保留了探索能力。

Conclusion: 通过熵感知的自适应方向控制，DASD有效解决了OPSD中探索与稳定性之间的权衡问题，实现了更优的推理性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [235] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的几何信号，通过测量客户端本地训练如何改变输入空间的激活诱导划分，来量化客户端与全局模型之间的功能偏离度，从而区分良性异构与异常偏离，实现风险感知的联邦学习聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构导致更新不稳定和全局性能下降，而实际部署中客户端更新不仅因非独立同分布而偏离，还可能因分布偏移或异常输入而偏离，现有方法难以有效区分这两种情况，亟需可靠的聚合可靠性监控手段。

Method: 提出一种置换不变、可解释的度量，不比较模型参数或梯度，而是通过共享探针集评估每个客户端局部训练如何改变输入空间的激活诱导划分，以此量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏差。

Result: 该度量能够有效识别引发异常功能变化的客户端，将稳定的异构客户端与显著偏离全局状态的客户端区分开来。

Conclusion: 该方法为联邦学习系统提供了一种监控客户端行为和实现风险感知聚合策略的简单工具。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [236] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel多会话咨询数据集和EmoTrack抑郁跟踪框架，结合LLM提取的临床信号与冻结的语义嵌入，在单会话和多会话场景下实现鲁棒的PHQ-8预测，在DAIC-WOZ上MAE降低13.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的心理健康咨询中，需要监控抑郁严重程度并及时标记需要人工干预的会话。但现有方法存在两难：微调方法在数据稀缺时泛化差，基于LLM的提示方法缺乏对跨会话上下文的支持。

Method: 提出EmoTrack框架：1) 使用LLM提取临床信号（症状级特征）；2) 结合冻结的逐轮语义嵌入；3) 训练症状特定预测器生成PHQ-8预测；4) 支持通过紧凑的跨会话记忆机制整合先前会话信息。

Result: 在LongCounsel和DAIC-WOZ上评估：单会话场景下，EmoTrack在DAIC-WOZ上相对最强基线MAE降低13.5%；在LongCounsel上，与最强纵向基线相比表现有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结合LLM临床信号提取与语义嵌入的方法能有效提升抑郁检测鲁棒性，兼顾数据效率与跨会话上下文建模能力。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [237] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重尾自正则化（HT-SR）理论的层级学习率（LLR）方法，根据各Transformer层的重尾性强弱自适应分配不同学习率，实验证明该方法在多种架构、优化器和参数规模下可实现1.5倍训练加速并提升零样本准确率（从47.09%提升至49.02%），且调优开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习主流做法对所有层使用统一学习率，忽略了Transformer各层结构的异质性，限制了LLM的训练效率与效果。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化理论，通过权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度量化各层的重尾性，对重尾性弱的层分配较大学习率以加速训练，对重尾性强的层分配较小学习率，实现跨层平衡训练。

Result: 在LLaMA到GPT-nano等多种架构、AdamW和Muon优化器、60M至1B参数规模上，LLR实现最高1.5倍训练加速，零样本平均准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且可以直接从统一基线迁移最优学习率设置，调优开销低。

Conclusion: 层级自适应学习率方法在多种场景下均优于统一学习率基线，训练速度更快、泛化性更好，是一种实用且高效的LLM训练策略。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [238] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SepsisAI-Orchestrator，一个用于早期脓毒症检测的开源模块化部署平台，解决了临床机器学习模型从研究到床旁应用的基础设施鸿沟。平台集成HL7 FHIR式预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM模型及Streamlit仪表盘，通过Docker/Kubernetes编排。负载测试发现副本数必须匹配物理CPU线程数，过度扩展反而因调度竞争导致性能下降，呈U型缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型虽预测能力强，但因数据表示异构、缺乏标准化部署流程、研究原型与医院环境并发/延迟需求不匹配等系统级障碍，难以转化为床旁实际应用。

Method: 构建开源模块化平台，包含HL7 FHIR启发式CDA预处理、NoSQL存储、经REST API服务的容器化LightGBM分类器、Streamlit临床仪表盘，通过Docker和Kubernetes编排。使用k6工具进行50-1000并发虚拟用户的负载测试，系统性地改变副本数量并测量p95延迟和请求失败率。

Result: 在12线程CPU上，副本从3扩展至12时p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s（降57.3%），所有请求失败消除；但过度扩展至24或48副本时，因调度竞争导致性能下降，呈现出此前在临床AI推理工作负载中未被量化的U型缩放行为。

Conclusion: 临床AI推理部署中副本数必须与主机物理CPU线程数匹配，过度扩展反而有害。平台证明了无需修改模型即可实现生产级部署，但未进行前瞻性临床验证。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load. Using k6 with 50-1000 concurrent virtual users, we find that replica count must be matched to the physical CPU thread count of the host: scaling from 3 to 12 replicas on a 12-thread CPU reduces p95 latency from 3.3s to 1.41s (57.3% reduction) and eliminates all request failures, while over-provisioning to 24 or 48 replicas degrades performance due to scheduler contention. To our knowledge this U-shaped scaling behavior has not been quantified previously for clinical AI inference workloads. We do not claim prospective clinical validation. Source code and deployment manifests are available at https://github.com/nucleusai/sepsisai-orchestrator.

</details>


### [239] [VeriScale: Adversarial Test-Suite Scaling for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22368)
*Yifan Bai,Xiaoyang Liu,Zihao Mou,Guihong Wang,Jian Yu,Shuhan Xie,Yantao Li,Yangyu Zhang,Jingwei Liang,Tao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriScale框架，通过对抗性实现驱动的测试套件扩展与缩减，构建高质量基准测试集，揭示LLM在代码规范生成与实现中的能力高估问题。基于该框架构建的VerinaPlus和VerinaLite在八种主流LLM上验证了其区分力与效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试因正负测试用例的数量和质量有限，导致对LLM在生成代码规范和实现方面的能力评估存在高估。

Method: 提出VeriScale框架，包含两阶段：测试套件扩展（通过对抗性实现构造多样化且具挑战性的测试用例）和测试套件缩减（蒸馏为紧凑但具区分力的测试套件）。实例化为VerinaPlus（扩展83倍）和VerinaLite（轻量级14倍变体）。

Result: 在八种SOTA LLM上，VerinaPlus揭示了原始基准隐藏的模型缺陷，SpecGen和CodeGen任务分数显著下降；VerinaLite以极低评估成本保持同等区分力。

Conclusion: VeriScale框架能有效构建高质量基准，更准确地评估LLM在软件工程任务中的真实能力，且其轻量变体兼具效率与区分力。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for software engineering, constructing high-quality benchmarks is crucial for evaluating not just the functional correctness, but also the formal verifiability of generated code. However, existing benchmarks are limited by the quantity and quality of positive and negative test cases, leading to an overestimation of model capabilities in generating specifications and implementations. To address this, we propose VeriScale, a novel framework driven by the adversarial implementations. It consists of two stages: test-suite expansion to construct diverse and challenging test cases, and test-suite reduction to distill them into compact yet discriminative suites. While VeriScale is general, we instantiate it on Verina to construct VerinaPlus, which expands the original test suites by over 83$\times$, and VerinaLite, a lightweight 14$\times$ variant. Our experiments across eight state-of-the-art LLMs demonstrate that VerinaPlus exposes substantial model weaknesses hidden by the original benchmark, evidenced by sharp score drops on both SpecGen and CodeGen tasks, whereas VerinaLite maintains this discriminative power at a fraction of the evaluation cost. The enhanced benchmarks and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/XiaoyangLiu-sjtu/VeriScale.

</details>


### [240] [Don't Forget the Critic: Value-Based Data Rehearsal for Multi-Cyclic Continual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22454)
*Benjamin Poole,Andrew Quinn,Li Yang,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多循环持续强化学习场景，提出Qreg+NWLU方法，通过连续数据排练和“No-Wait”正则化两个简单改进，在价值函数近似框架下有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，提升了学习效率和知识迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续强化学习（CRL）中的数据排练方法局限于策略梯度框架，仅对actor进行正则化而忽略critic正则化带来的性能下降，且现有评估很少考虑多循环环境（任务序列重复），而这种现实场景会加剧遗忘和塑性损失。

Method: 提出Qreg+NWLU方法，基于Deep Q-Networks（DQN）在价值函数近似设置中引入两项改进：(1) 连续数据排练——在训练过程中动态收集并更新存储的Q值；(2) "No-Wait"正则化——在第一个任务结束后立即应用正则化，而非等待后续任务。

Result: 相比传统的Qreg方法和常规CRL方法，Qreg+NWLU在学习效率、遗忘缓解和知识迁移方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 数据排练在价值函数近似框架下同样有效，简单的连续排练和即时正则化策略就能在多循环持续学习场景中带来显著性能提升，为CRL中价值函数的正则化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data rehearsal has emerged as a leading approach for mitigating catastrophic forgetting in Continual Reinforcement Learning (CRL). However, existing work remains confined to policy gradient frameworks, regularizing only actors due to the performance degradation incurred by critic regularization. This actor-centric approach overlooks the potential of data rehearsal for value function approximation. Moreover, existing evaluations in CRL rarely consider multi-cyclic environments where task sequences repeat, a critical real-world scenario that exacerbates forgetting and plasticity. We investigate data rehearsal for Deep Q-Networks using Q-value regularization in multi-cyclic settings and propose Qreg+NWLU which introduces two simple modifications: (1) continuous data rehearsal that dynamically collects and updates stored Q-values throughout training, and (2) "No-Wait" regularization that applies immediately rather than after the first task. Together, these modifications yield improvements in learning efficiency, forgetting mitigation, and knowledge transfer over Qreg and conventional CRL methods within value function approximation settings.

</details>


### [241] [Understanding Multimodal Failure in Action-Chunking Behavioral Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22493)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Massimiliano Datres,Ariel Rodriguez,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Gitta Kutyniok,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了行为克隆中观测对应多个有效动作的多模态问题，分析了不同参数化策略（潜变量策略和动作空间生成策略）在多模态行为克隆中的失败机制，并通过实验验证了这些机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在相同观测对应多个有效动作时变得困难，现有方法未能充分理解不同多模态参数化策略失败的原因。

Method: 分析了两种多模态参数化策略：(1) 潜变量策略中后验-先验正则化对部署采样可靠性与模态信息保持的权衡；(2) 动作空间生成策略中基空间到动作空间映射的Lipschitz常数对多模态覆盖能力的约束。

Result: 过度正则化会移除区分示范模态所需的动作条件信息；减少正则化可保留模态信息但依赖先验覆盖相关潜区域。动作空间生成策略中，小Lipschitz常数无法为多个分离模态分配高概率，覆盖多模态需要基空间的锐利变换或动作空间的离支撑桥接区域。

Conclusion: 不同多模态参数化策略存在不同的失败模式，合成多模态任务和机器人仿真基准的实验结果支持所提出的机制。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning becomes difficult when the same observation admits several valid actions. We study this problem for action-chunking policies and show that different multimodal parameterizations fail in different ways. For latent-variable policies, posterior-prior regularization makes deployment-time sampling more reliable, but excessive regularization removes the action-conditioned information needed to distinguish demonstrated modes. Reducing this regularization can preserve mode information, but then success depends on whether the prior covers the relevant latent regions. For action-space generative policies, multimodality is constrained by the smoothness of the base-to-action transport: a map with small Lipschitz constant cannot assign substantial probability to many well-separated modes. Covering many modes therefore requires either sharp transitions in base space or off-support bridge regions in action space. Experiments on synthetic multimodal tasks and robotic simulation benchmarks support these mechanisms.

</details>


### [242] [An Evidence Hierarchy for Bayesian Object Classification via OSINT-Aided Heterogeneous Sensor Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22259)
*Jan Nausner,Michael Hubner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文感知与领域知识增强的异构传感器融合方法，用于CBRNE威胁检测、定位与分类。通过建立新型证据层次结构、引入OSINT环境信息、以及基于贝叶斯分类与领域知识先验，在模拟场景中实现了最高95%的分类准确率，并展现出对杂波和先验失配的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一传感器仅能检测部分威胁且可靠性不一，传感器端高杂波率以及高质量数据集的缺乏，使得CBRNE威胁的检测与分类面临巨大挑战。

Method: 1) 建立直接、指示性和情境性三层证据层次结构；2) 通过OSINT数据采集与处理引入环境上下文信息；3) 基于贝叶斯分类机制，利用领域知识构建先验分布进行威胁分类。

Result: 模拟实验中，所提方法在前景杂波和先验失配条件下表现出鲁棒性，整体分类准确率最高达95%。

Conclusion: 上下文感知与领域知识增强的融合方法能有效弥补传感器不足，提升复杂环境下CBRNE威胁分类的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous sensor fusion is vital for detecting, localizing, and classifying CBRNE threats. However, individual sensors are often only capable of detecting a subset of relevant threats with varying reliability or can even provide only indirect threat indications, making threat classification challenging. Furthermore, high clutter rates on the sensor side present a great challenge for fusion systems. Additionally, the limited availability of high quality datasets hinders the advancement of learning-based detection and classification models in smart sensors. To mitigate these sensor related shortcomings, a context-aware and domain knowledge-enhanced fusion process is proposed. First, a novel evidence hierarchy is established that enables modeling of direct, indicative, and contextual information. Second, contextual information about the environment is introduced into the fusion process, by collecting, processing, and exploiting OSINT inputs. Third, all levels of the evidence hierarchy are used to craft a Bayesian threat type classification mechanism with domain knowledge-informed priors. The proposed methodology is evaluated in simulated scenarios, and the results demonstrate the benefit of the proposed fusion approach in terms of robustness to clutter and prior mismatch, with an overall classification accuracy of up to 95%.

</details>


### [243] [Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22711)
*Clarisse Wibault,Alexander Goldie,Antonio Villares,Maike Osborne,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在离线目标条件强化学习中，层次化策略不仅通过时间抽象减小决策视界，还能通过相对化选项和分层表示实现绝对抽象，从而跨相似状态-目标对复用经验，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中，马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）常因状态-目标对间的对称性和共享结构而存在显著冗余，现有方法未能充分利用这种结构。

Method: 引入相对化选项（relativised options）以及为层次结构中不同层级使用不同表示，使智能体能够跨相似上下文复用经验；基于此框架提出两种简单算法，分别用于学习相对化选项和从绝对参考系中抽象。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的归纳偏置在离线GCRL中显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: 层次化策略除了降低时间维度的复杂性外，还能通过相对化抽象实现空间/状态维度的结构复用，为离线GCRL提供了一种有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.

</details>


### [244] [The Double Dilemma in Multi-Task Radiology Report Generation: A Gradient Dynamics Analysis and Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22635)
*Erjian Zhang,Yatong Hao,Liejun Wang,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多任务学习在放射学报告生成（RRG）中线性标量化策略导致的梯度动态失衡问题（漂移项偏差与扩散项衰减的“双重困境”），本文提出主干无关的优化器CAME-Grad，通过冲突规避方向校正与幅度增强能量注入确保几何有效性并避免局部最优，再通过自适应梯度融合机制动态平衡理论最优方向与任务归纳偏置。实验表明该即插即用优化器在8种RRG方法上带来一致且显著的性能提升，MIMIC-CXR和IU X-Ray上临床效能平均提升2.3%和1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多任务学习的自动放射学报告生成（RRG）方法大多采用粗粒度的线性标量化策略，无法有效平衡判别性临床监督的硬约束与报告生成的平滑性要求，导致性能受限。本文从梯度动力学角度分析线性标量化的失败机制，利用随机微分方程（SDE）框架将其表征为“双重困境”——漂移项偏差与扩散项衰减，从而提出针对性解决方案。

Method: 提出主干无关的优化器CAME-Grad，核心包括：(1) 冲突规避方向校正（conflict-averse direction rectification）与幅度增强能量注入（magnitude-enhanced energy injection），确保梯度几何有效性并避免局部最优解；(2) 自适应梯度融合机制，动态平衡理论最优方向与任务特定归纳偏置。

Result: 作为通用即插即用优化器，CAME-Grad在8种不同的RRG方法上带来一致且显著的性能提升，在MIMIC-CXR上临床效能平均提升2.3%，在IU X-Ray上平均提升1.9%。

Conclusion: CAME-Grad有效解决了多任务RRG中线性标量化的梯度动态失衡问题，是一种能够广泛提升现有RRG方法临床效能的通用优化策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: While multi-task learning based automatic radiology report generation (RRG) is widely adopted to ensure clinical consistency, most focus on architectural designs yet remain limited to coarse linear scalarization strategies. These strategies cannot effectively balance the hard constraints of discriminative clinical supervision with the smoothness requirements of report generation. To address these problems, we analyze the failure mechanism of linear scalarization from the perspective of gradient dynamics, utilizing the stochastic differential equation (SDE) framework to characterize it as a "Double Dilemma" of drift term deviation and diffusion term decay. Based on this, we propose a backbone-agnostic optimizer named Conflict-Averse Magnitude-Enhanced Gradient Descent (CAME-Grad). Through conflict-averse direction rectification and magnitude-enhanced energy injection, the algorithm not only ensures geometric validity, but also avoids local optimal solutions. Then, the adaptive gradient fusion mechanism is used to establish a dynamic balance between the theoretical optimal direction and the task-specific inductive bias. Experiments show that as a universal plug-and-play optimizer, CAME-Grad brings substantial and consistent improvements across eight diverse RRG methods, elevating overall clinical efficacy performance by an average of 2.3\% on MIMIC-CXR and 1.9\% on IU X-Ray. Our code is available at https://github.com/vpsg-research/CAME-Grad.

</details>


### [245] [The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22723)
*Md Sahil Akhtar,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias,Adam D. Jozefiak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用完整后验协方差匹配替代各向同性协方差，将DDPM反向路径KL散度从Ω(1/T)降至O(1/T²)，并设计了无需训练的Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），通过协方差-向量乘积高效实现，仅需3次Lanczos步骤即可超越强对角协方差基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯DDPM中，标准各向同性反向协方差在路径空间KL散度上存在不可避免的Ω(1/T)误差，这限制了分类器引导等需要对整个反向轨迹进行扰动的过程的效果。

Method: 提出匹配完整后验协方差以降低路径KL至O(1/T²)；引入Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），一种无训练、无矩阵的采样方法，通过协方差-向量乘积（利用后验均值的雅可比-向量乘积实现）从最优反向协方差中采样，避免稠密存储和额外协方差模型。

Result: 理论上证明LGS近似误差随Lanczos步骤数指数衰减；实验上仅用3步Lanczos即可在标准图像基准上超越OCM-DDPM等强对角协方差基线。

Conclusion: 完整协方差匹配不仅在理论上更具优势，在实际快速DDPM采样中也具有可行性和实用性。

Abstract: A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.

</details>


### [246] [CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22774)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogAdapt框架，将临床心电图基础模型适配到可穿戴设备的认知负荷评估任务，通过LeadBridge适配器将3导联信号转换为12导联表示，并结合ProFine渐进式微调策略，在两个公开数据集上显著优于从头训练的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时认知负荷评估对自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临标注数据有限和跨被试泛化性差的问题。现有临床心电图基础模型虽具有丰富表征能力，但由于传感器配置不匹配和任务差异，无法直接应用于可穿戴设备。

Method: 提出CogAdapt框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) LeadBridge——可学习适配器，将3导联可穿戴信号转换为解剖学一致的12导联表示；(2) ProFine——渐进式微调策略，逐步解冻编码器层并防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公开数据集上采用留一被试交叉验证，CogAdapt分别取得0.626和0.768的macro-F1分数，显著优于从头训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明基础模型适配策略在可穿戴传感器的跨被试认知负荷评估中具有良好前景，为临床预训练模型向边缘设备迁移提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.

</details>


### [247] [MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22775)
*Amir Mousavi,Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani,Erfan Nourbakhsh,Mimi Xie,Rocky Slavin,Leslie Neely,John Davis,John Quarles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MambaGaze框架，通过XMD编码处理眼动数据缺失问题，利用双向Mamba-2以线性复杂度建模长时间依赖，在认知负荷评估任务中超越CNN/Transformer等基线方法4-12个百分点，并在边缘设备上实现实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现基于眼动信号的实时认知负荷评估对自适应人机交互至关重要，但面临两个挑战：频繁的数据缺失（眨眼、追踪失败）以及高效建模长时间序列依赖关系。

Method: MambaGaze框架包含两个核心组件：1) XMD编码，对原始眼动特征进行观测掩码和时间差增强，显式建模数据不确定性；2) 双向Mamba-2模块，以线性计算复杂度捕获时间依赖关系。

Result: 在CLARE和CL-Drive数据集上，留一被试交叉验证下分别达到76.8%和73.1%准确率，超越CNN、Transformer、ResNet、VGG基线4-12个百分点；在NVIDIA Jetson边缘平台上实现43-68 FPS实时推理，功耗低于7.5W。

Conclusion: MambaGaze有效解决了眼动数据缺失和长程依赖建模两大挑战，在准确性和边缘部署效率上均表现出色，具备在安全关键场景中用于可穿戴认知负荷监测的可行性。

Abstract: Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.

</details>


### [248] [Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22817)
*Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Isha Puri,Idan Shenfeld,Akarsh Kumar,Mehul Damani,Sebastian Risi,Omar Khattab,Zhang-Wei Hong,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出向量策略优化(VPO)算法，将RL训练从优化单一标量奖励扩展为优化向量奖励空间，使LLM在推理时搜索中产生更多样化的解决方案，在代码生成等任务中超越标量RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM后训练范式优化预定义的标量奖励，导致模型产生低熵响应分布，缺乏推理时搜索所需的多样性，无法适应AlphaEvolve等需要多样化rollout的搜索过程。

Method: VPO是GRPO优势估计器的即插即用替代方案，利用实际任务中奖励往往是向量值（如代码生成中每个测试用例的正确性、多个用户画像或奖励模型）的特点，训练LLM输出一组解，其中个体解专门针对向量奖励空间中的不同权衡。

Result: 在四个任务上，VPO在测试时搜索（如pass@k和best@k）中匹配或超越最强标量RL基线，且随搜索预算增加差距扩大。在进化搜索中，VPO模型能解决GRPO模型完全无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 随着测试时搜索变得越来越标准化，优化多样性可能需要成为默认的后训练目标。

Abstract: Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.

</details>
